(JCI Insight). scRNA-seq of malignant effusions from transformed SCLC revealing NE and non-NE subsets, with MEK inhibitor plus chemotherapy active in vitro and in vivo; mechanistic insight into histologic transformation with a therapeutic angle. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41574603/
- Overcoming EGFR resistance by monovalent and bident inhibitors targeting Cys775. (bioRxiv). Preprint, not peer reviewed. Preprint (not peer reviewed) describing first-in-class covalent EGFR Cys775 inhibitor ZNL-3 active in osimertinib-resistant mouse models and a dual C775/C797 bident inhibitor; preclinical next-generation EGFR inhibitor. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41573874/
- An alternative EGFR activation by patient-derived R252C mutation promotes cancer progression. (Nat Commun). Nature Communications mechanistic study of a patient-derived extracellular EGFR R252C mutation causing alternative EGFR activation; mechanistic with possible treatment relevance. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41565660/
- Endostar overcomes Osimertinib-resistance mediated EGFR-activation mutation, T790M and cis-C797S triple mutations via enhancing the binding of Osimertinib to EGFR protein. (Life Sci). Nine-patient case series plus preclinical data suggesting Endostar plus osimertinib for EGFR activating/T790M/cis-C797S triple mutations (ORR 66.7%, median PFS 8.9 months); post-osimertinib clinical signal but very small and uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41564925/
- A genome-wide genetic screen reveals the P2Y2-integrin axis as a stabilizer of EGFR mutants in non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC). (Sci Adv). Genome-wide RNAi screen identifying the P2Y2-integrin axis as a stabiliser of mutant EGFR; mechanistic with a potential therapeutic target. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41564171/
- Spread through alveolar space predicts recurrence in epidermal growth factor receptor-mutated pathological stage IA lung adenocarcinoma. (Jpn J Clin Oncol). Retrospective cohort of 856 resected stage IA EGFR-mutated LUAD showing STAS predicts recurrence (5-year CIR 14.4% vs 4.2%); relevant to adjuvant selection. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41563067/
- Phase 1/2 trial of brigatinib plus panitumumab in patients with osimertinib-resistant EGFR-mutated non-small cell lung cancer harboring EGFR C797S mutation. (Cancer Treat Res Commun). Phase 1/2 trial of brigatinib plus panitumumab in osimertinib-resistant C797S NSCLC, stopped after 5 patients (all PD, median PFS 1.7 months, 3 early pulmonary events); negative post-osimertinib data but tiny. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41558224/
- Validation of histopathology-based deep learning algorithms for detection of actionable non-small cell lung cancer biomarkers. (NPJ Precis Oncol). Independent validation of H&E-based deep-learning classifiers for EGFR, ALK, BRAF and MET alterations; validated biomarker-detection tool with potential testing implications. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41554849/
- Zongertinib: First Approval. (Drugs). Drug profile of zongertinib's first (accelerated) FDA approval for previously treated HER2-mutant NSCLC based on phase Ia/Ib Beamion LUNG-1; relevant lung approval but HER2-mutant rather than EGFR-directed and a summary review. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41549172/
- Prognostic value of preoperative ctDNA and pathological venous invasion for recurrence in EGFR-mutated non-small cell lung cancer. (Lung Cancer). Single-centre cohort of 88 resected EGFR-mutant NSCLC: preoperative ctDNA positivity (26%) associated with lower 5-year DFS (54% vs 84%, HR 3.25) and OS; ctDNA-relevant but small and retrospective with wide CIs. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41546895/
- Evaluating the efficacy and safety of antibody-drug conjugates in non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (BMC Cancer). Systematic review and meta-analysis of prospective phase II-IV ADC trials in NSCLC, including subgroups; useful synthesis but mixes single-arm and randomised trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41545863/
- Targeting driver mutations in lung cancer with interstitial pneumonia: A nationwide study in Japan. (Eur J Cancer). Japanese nationwide multicentre study of driver testing, driver frequency and targeted-therapy safety/efficacy in NSCLC with interstitial pneumonia; real-world cohort relevant to safety of EGFR-TKIs. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41539179/
- Minimal Residual Disease Enhances Prognostic Stratification beyond Pathologic Response in Resectable Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Clin Cancer Res). Exploratory MRD analysis (Guardant Reveal) in 60 patients from randomised NADIM II perioperative chemoimmunotherapy trial; MRD added prognostic value beyond pCR (EFS HR 10.2), but small subset and EGFR/ALK-negative population. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41537692/
- Prevalence of Genomic Alterations in EGFR, KRAS, NRAS, and BRAF Among Early-Stage Nonsquamous Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer in Brazil. (JCO Glob Oncol). Brazilian cohort of 557 early-stage nonsquamous NSCLC reporting EGFR/KRAS/BRAF/NRAS prevalence; regional epidemiology relevant to access/testing differences. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41538756/
- EGFR activation disrupts immunotherapy response via SHP2-mediated suppression of tumor-intrinsic response to IFN-γ. (J Clin Invest). Mechanistic JCI study showing EGFR activation suppresses tumour-intrinsic IFN-gamma response via SHP2, explaining poor ICB response in EGFR-driven tumours; clear clinical angle but preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41538362/
- EGFR inhibitor-resistant lung cancers exhibit collateral sensitivity to a covalent, cysteine-independent KEAP1 oligomerizing molecular bridge. (Nat Commun). Nat Commun preclinical study showing diverse EGFR-inhibitor-resistant NSCLC models are hypersensitive to KEAP1-oligomerising compound MCB-613; mechanistic insight into resistance but tool compound without clinical path. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41535280/
- Mutational landscape of lung adenocarcinoma in Czechia. (Cancer Treat Res Commun). Panel sequencing of 1218 lung adenocarcinomas from Czechia giving EGFR (18.8%) and KRAS frequencies; regional data relevant to between-country differences. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41529505/
- Telehealth-delivered exercise and nutrition intervention to improve outcomes in patients with early stage non-small cell lung cancer: protocol for the multicentre STARLighT phase II (neoadjuvant) and phase III (adjuvant) trial. (BMJ Open). Protocol for the multicentre STARLighT phase II/III telehealth exercise and nutrition trial in early-stage NSCLC; protocol of a major trial, no outcome data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41526018/
- Prevalence of EGFR mutations in patients with resected stage I-III non-small cell lung cancer: results from EARLY-EGFR Latin America study. (J Thorac Dis). Prospective observational EARLY-EGFR Latin America subset reporting EGFR mutation prevalence in resected stage I-III NSCLC; regional data relevant to adjuvant testing and access. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41522158/
- Differential prevalence and prognostic significance of spread through air spaces according to oncogenic driver mutations in lung adenocarcinoma. (J Thorac Cardiovasc Surg). Large surgical cohort (n=4027) showing spread-through-air-spaces prevalence and prognostic impact differ by driver mutation including EGFR; retrospective single-institution pathology study. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41520721/
- Survival outcomes of patients with uncommon EGFR mutations in surgically resected lung adenocarcinoma: A multi-institutional real-world database study (CReGYT-01 EGFR study). (Lung Cancer). Japanese 21-institution retrospective cohort (n=1636 resected EGFR-mutant LUAD): uncommon mutations had worse OS (HR 1.54) and LCSS than common mutations, driven by exon 20 insertions; large real-world cohort, retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41512381/
- Targeting common EGFR mutations in non-small cell lung cancer: a review of global phase 3 trials of third-generation inhibitors. (J Natl Cancer Inst). JNCI critical review of global phase 3 trials of third-generation EGFR TKIs for common EGFR mutations; synthesis of key trial evidence but no new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41512290/
- A study protocol of a single-arm, prospective, open-label, non-controlled, phase II study of neoadjuvant BL-B01D1 combined with aumolertinib in resectable stage II-IIIB non-small cell lung cancer patients with EGFR mutation. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Protocol for a single-arm phase II (n=40) of neoadjuvant EGFR/HER3 bispecific ADC BL-B01D1 plus aumolertinib in resectable EGFR-mutant NSCLC; new agent but no results yet. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41510386/
- Efficacy and safety of Osimertinib and other third-generation EGFR TKIs in advanced NSCLC: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Discov Oncol). Systematic review and meta-analysis of RCTs of osimertinib and other third-generation EGFR TKIs in advanced NSCLC; adds limited new information beyond existing trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41501563/
- Efficacy and Safety of Firmonertinib Plus Anlotinib as First-Line Treatment for Advanced NSCLC With EGFR Mutations: A Single-Arm, Phase I/II Trial. (Clin Lung Cancer). Single-arm phase I/II (n=36) of first-line firmonertinib plus anlotinib in EGFR-mutant NSCLC: ORR 63.9%, median PFS 19.6 months, grade>=3 TRAEs 36%; small uncontrolled trial. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41500871/
- A remarkable year for NSCLC: Seven new FDA approvals in 2025 across molecular targets. (Biomol Biomed). Editorial summarising seven 2025 FDA approvals in NSCLC including EGFR exon 20 and TROP2/MET ADCs; useful approval overview but no new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41499692/
- The NGS and PCR-based Detection of EGFR Mutations in Liquid Biopsy: A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis Compared With Tissue Biopsy in Treatment-naïve Patients With Non-small Cell Lung Cancer. (Cancer Diagn Progn). Systematic review and meta-analysis of NGS/PCR liquid-biopsy EGFR detection vs tissue in treatment-naive NSCLC; relevant to ctDNA testing but diagnostic-accuracy synthesis only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41487925/
- Efficacy and safety of later-line targeted therapies in advanced non-small cell lung cancer with EGFR exon 20 insertion mutations: a systematic review. (Front Pharmacol). Systematic review of later-line targeted therapies (amivantamab, mobocertinib, sunvozertinib) for EGFR exon 20 insertion NSCLC; synthesis of mostly single-arm trial data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41487495/
- An updated cost-effectiveness analysis of osimertinib versus placebo in resected EGFR-mutated non-small cell lung cancer: the perspective of the United States. (BMC Health Serv Res). Updated US cost-effectiveness analysis of adjuvant osimertinib after ADAURA OS data; relevant to access but model-based. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41484770/
- Liquid biopsy and tissue biopsy for the detection of EGFR mutations in patients with stage III non-small-cell lung cancer: an observational real-world study. (ESMO Open). ESMO Open real-world observational study of liquid vs tissue biopsy for EGFR detection in stage III NSCLC; relevant to ctDNA testing in non-metastatic disease, retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41483627/
- Role of Oral Tetracyclines in Preventing Acneiform Rash in Patients With Non-small Cell Lung Cancer Treated With Epidermal Growth Factor Receptor Tyrosine Kinase Inhibitors: A Systematic Review. (Actas Dermosifiliogr). Systematic review of oral tetracycline prophylaxis for EGFR-TKI acneiform rash; relevant to tolerance but limited evidence base. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41482263/
- A tumor-derived lactate/ENO1 lactylation feedback loop facilitates osimertinib resistance of lung adenocarcinoma. (Drug Resist Updat). Proteomics of patient LUAD tissue plus functional work identifying a lactate/ENO1 lactylation loop driving osimertinib resistance; mechanistic with potential therapeutic angle but preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41478201/
- Cost comparison of osimertinib plus platinum-pemetrexed versus amivantamab plus lazertinib for the first-line treatment of patients with locally advanced or metastatic epidermal growth factor receptor-mutated non-small cell lung cancer. (J Med Econ). Cost comparison of first-line osimertinib-chemotherapy vs IV/SC amivantamab-lazertinib in EGFR-mutant NSCLC; relevant to access but model-based (likely sponsor-driven). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41973054/
- Second Line Treatment Decision as Per Standard of Care or Foundation Medicine in Patients With Locally Advanced or Metastatic Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Cancer Med). Spanish multicentre Lung-ONE study of comprehensive genomic profiling guiding second-line NSCLC decisions; relevant to testing/access but descriptive. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41968649/
- Evaluating Tumor Burden as a Predictive Biomarker for Epidermal Growth Factor Receptor Targeted Kinase Inhibitor Therapy in Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (JCO Precis Oncol). Stanford real-world cohort (n=312) of first-line EGFR-TKI: bone metastasis and number of metastatic organs predicted worse OS (3-year OS 59.8% vs 41.5% low vs high burden); retrospective single-institution, hypothesis-generating for treatment intensification. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41955549/
- Targeted therapy in the treatment of lung cancer in Iceland 2010-2023. (Acta Oncol). Nationwide Icelandic registry study (n=2528, 2010-2023) of molecular testing uptake (EGFR 9.9% of tested) and targeted-therapy use; Nordic real-world access data, descriptive. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41954526/
- Structure-guided discovery of a potent 2-aryl-4-aminoquinazoline-based inhibitor overcoming osimertinib resistance driven by EGFR C797S mutation in NSCLC. (Eur J Med Chem). Structure-guided fourth-generation EGFR inhibitor 6g active against Del19/T790M/C797S with xenograft TGI 64% vs 16% for osimertinib; preclinical C797S agent with in vivo data but no clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41950648/
- ADC Target Profiling in NSCLC: Generalizable AI Separates TROP-2 and cMET Phenotypes. (Clin Cancer Res). AI pipeline for quantifying TROP-2 and cMET ADC target expression in NSCLC; relevant to ADC biomarker selection but not linked to treatment outcomes. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41945491/
- Effectiveness of amivantamab compared with real-world therapies in patients with non-small cell lung cancer with EGFR exon 20 insertion mutations post platinum-based chemotherapy. (J Formos Med Assoc). Taiwanese external-control comparison of amivantamab (CHRYSALIS) vs real-world therapies in post-platinum EGFR exon 20 insertion NSCLC; non-randomised comparison. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41945065/
- Furmonertinib combined with bevacizumab in EGFR-TKI-resistant leptomeningeal metastasis: analysis of the CSF ctDNA molecular response and survival outcomes. (Br J Cancer). Real-world cohort (n=104) with leptomeningeal metastasis after third-generation TKI: high-dose furmonertinib plus bevacizumab vs furmonertinib alone associated with longer iPFS (6.8 vs 4.0 mo) and OS (15.3 vs 7.1 mo), with CSF ctDNA response prognostic; relevant CNS data but non-randomised and retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41942607/
- Adaptive Regulation of dNTP Homeostasis Confers Osimertinib Resistance in EGFR-Mutant Non-Small Cell Lung Carcinoma. (Cancer Res). Cancer Res mechanistic study: osimertinib-induced dNTP depletion triggers a CHK2-TNNT3-RRM2B compensatory axis, and CHK2 inhibition delayed resistance in vitro and in xenografts; actionable preclinical resistance mechanism. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41941751/
- Taxanes Versus Pemetrexed After Osimertinib Resistance in EGFR-Mutated NSCLC: A Retrospective Cohort with Two-Model In Vitro Validation. (Cancer Manag Res). Retrospective propensity-matched comparison of taxane vs pemetrexed chemotherapy after osimertinib resistance: no significant overall difference (PFS HR 0.71), benefit for taxanes in dramatic progression subgroup; retrospective, subgroup-driven. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41940256/
- Efficacy and Safety of First-Line Ramucirumab Plus Erlotinib for EGFR L858R-Mutated NSCLC in Real-World Practice: A Retrospective Multicenter REAL-SPEED Analysis. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Retrospective multicentre REAL-SPEED cohort (n=168) of first-line ramucirumab plus erlotinib in L858R NSCLC: median PFS 17.4 mo, OS 35.6 mo; real-world data, uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41938081/
- Lobar vs Sublobar Lung Resection for Early-Stage Epidermal Growth Factor Receptor-Mutant Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Ann Thorac Surg). National hospital dataset (n=6918) with propensity matching: sublobar and lobar resection gave similar 3-year OS in EGFR-mutant tumours <=2 cm (95.3% vs 93.7%), but sublobar was worse in EGFR wild-type; retrospective, short follow-up. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41933780/
- Real-world demographics, clinical characteristics, and testing and treatment patterns in patients with non-small-cell lung cancer and extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer in Belgium: the AIBED study. (ESMO Real World Data Digit Oncol). Belgian multicentre real-world AIBED study of testing and treatment patterns in NSCLC and ES-SCLC; relevant to country-level access/testing, descriptive. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41930294/
- ROCK1 inhibition primes anti-tumor immunity in EGFR-mutant NSCLC by triggering PD-L1 degradation mediated by SMURF2. (Mol Cancer). Mol Cancer preclinical study showing ROCK1 inhibition degrades PD-L1 and combined with PD-L1 blockade is active in EGFR-mutant NSCLC mouse models; mechanistic with clinical angle, preclinical only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41928228/
- Synergistic Antitumor Activity of Combination Therapy with a MET TKI Vabametkib and a Third-Generation EGFR TKI Lazertinib in MET-Amplified EGFR-Mutant NSCLC. (Cancer Res Treat). Preclinical study of vabametkib plus lazertinib in MET-amplified osimertinib-resistant cell lines, organoid and two PDX models showing synergy; credible path (ongoing phase II NCT05541822). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41927036/
- National Delphi consensus on perioperative and locally advanced NSCLC management in Colombia. (Expert Rev Anticancer Ther). Colombian national Delphi consensus on perioperative and stage III NSCLC (33/34 statements agreed, osimertinib consolidation favoured in EGFR-mutant stage III); regional consensus largely restating existing evidence. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41921504/
- [Real-life first-line systemic treatments for metastatic non-small cell lung cancer (mNSCLC) - Data from the KBP-2020-CPHG cohort]. (Bull Cancer). French prospective observational KBP-2020 cohort describing real-life first-line treatment of metastatic NSCLC; large national real-world data, descriptive and mainly non-driver disease. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41916832/
- Factors Associated With Long-Term Survivors With Epidermal Growth Factor Receptor Positive Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer Treated With First or Second-Generation Tyrosine Kinase Inhibitors: A Canadian Multi-Center Cohort. (Asia Pac J Clin Oncol). Canadian multicentre real-world cohort (n=577) of first/second-generation TKIs: exon 19 deletion and East-Asian ancestry independently associated with longer survival; retrospective, older TKIs. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41913333/
- The Efficacy and Safety of Tislelizumab plus Anlotinib as First-line Treatment in Advanced Pulmonary Sarcomatoid Carcinoma: A Single-Arm Phase II Trial. (Clin Cancer Res). Prospective single-arm phase II of first-line tislelizumab plus anlotinib in advanced pulmonary sarcomatoid carcinoma (EGFR/ALK-negative); phase 2 trial in rare subtype, uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41894181/
- Comparative Efficacy and Safety of First-Line Immune Checkpoint Inhibitors Plus Chemotherapy with or Without Bevacizumab in Advanced Non-Squamous Non-Small Cell Lung Carcinoma. (Curr Oncol). Real-world comparison plus network meta-analysis of chemoimmunotherapy with vs without bevacizumab in non-squamous NSCLC; indirect comparison, driver-negative population. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41892201/
- Sevabertinib: First Approval. (Drugs). Drug profile of sevabertinib's first (accelerated) FDA approval for previously treated HER2-mutant NSCLC; relevant lung approval but HER2-directed and a summary review. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41882473/
- TAS3351 is a brain penetrable EGFR-TKI that overcomes T790M and C797S resistant mutations. (Commun Med (Lond)). Preclinical evaluation of fourth-generation TKI TAS3351 active against T790M/C797S, sparing wild-type EGFR, brain-penetrant with intracranial efficacy in mice; promising agent but no clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41882274/
- CHIO3: CHemotherapy combined with immune checkpoint inhibitor for operable stage IIIA/B (N2) Non-Small cell lung cancer (AFT-46). (Lung Cancer). Single-arm phase II CHIO3 (n=37) of neoadjuvant chemo-durvalumab in N2 NSCLC (EGFR excluded): N2 nodal clearance 73.3%, pCR 30%; small uncontrolled perioperative trial. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41880691/
- Immunological effects of amivantamab in EGFR or MET-expressing non-small cell lung cancer. (Cancer Immunol Immunother). Ex vivo study in 40 resected NSCLC showing amivantamab activates CD8+ T cells and dendritic cells, most in high EGFR/MET-expressing tumours; mechanistic with clinical angle, small and ex vivo. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41874698/
- EGFR G719X + S768I co-mutations in NSCLC: genomic landscape and differential responses to EGFR-TKIs in a large real-world cohort. (BMC Cancer). Retrospective cohort of 142 patients with EGFR G719X+S768I co-mutation: afatinib gave higher ORR (76.8%) and longer PFS (23.4 mo) than first- or third-generation TKIs; useful uncommon-mutation data but retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41872782/
- Coronavirus disease 2019 infection reduces EGFR-TKI efficacy in non-small cell lung carcinoma: Real-world evidence from a multicenter propensity-matched cohort. (Int J Cancer). Multicentre Chinese propensity-matched cohort (n=711; 426 matched) suggesting COVID-19 infection associated with shorter first-line EGFR-TKI PFS (18.2 vs 12.9 mo); retrospective with historical comparator. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41863042/
- Real-World Outcomes of Sequential Afatinib and Osimertinib Versus Afatinib and Chemotherapy in EGFR-Mutant NSCLC: Taiwan Multicenter GIANT Study. (Target Oncol). Taiwanese multicentre retrospective GIANT cohort (n=733): afatinib followed by osimertinib had median OS 55 vs 32.3 months for other sequences (aHR 0.43); sequencing data but strongly confounded by T790M selection. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41862776/
- Early success of the COCOON trial: Preventing dermatologic adverse events in first-line EGFR-mutant NSCLC. (Oncoscience). Commentary on interim phase II COCOON results: dermatologic prophylaxis halved grade>=2 skin AEs (38.6% vs 76.5%) with first-line amivantamab-lazertinib; secondary report of conference data, not the primary publication. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41859193/
- Clinical Outcomes and Safety of Concurrent Chemoradiotherapy in EGFR-Mutant Unresectable Stage III NSCLC. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Pooled analysis of three randomised phase II Japanese trials (n=274): EGFR-mutant stage III NSCLC had shorter PFS after concurrent chemoradiotherapy (HR 1.38) and more grade 3-4 pneumonitis (7.8% vs 2.6%), similar OS; secondary analysis, pre-LAURA era. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41847154/
- Safety evaluation of afatinib in patients with a history of interstitial lung disease using data from an administrative claims database in Japan. (Int J Clin Pharm). Japanese claims database study of 2174 afatinib-treated patients: severe ILD in 6.0% of those with prior ILD; useful safety signal, limited by claims-based definitions. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41843044/
- When EGFR meets MET: Dual blockade as the next post-TKI standard? (Med). Commentary in Med on phase 3 SACHI (savolitinib plus osimertinib vs chemotherapy after EGFR-TKI failure in MET-amplified NSCLC, PFS 9.8 vs 5.4 mo, HR 0.34); relevant post-TKI evidence but commentary, not the primary report. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41831430/
- EGFR mutation status, treatment patterns, and outcomes in resectable early-stage non-small cell lung cancer prior to adjuvant EGFR-TKI approval: An international real-world study. (Lung Cancer). International multicentre real-world study of EGFR mutation status, treatment and outcomes in resected early-stage NSCLC before adjuvant TKI approval; useful baseline across countries, retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41825181/
- Efficacy and safety of neoadjuvant targeted therapy in non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Syst Rev). Systematic review and meta-analysis of neoadjuvant EGFR-TKI trials in resectable EGFR-mutant NSCLC; synthesis of small heterogeneous trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41821091/
- Targeting tumor suppressor gene co-mutations in EGFR-mutant nonsmall cell lung cancer: How ACROSS2 sharpens the case for early intensification. (CA Cancer J Clin). CA Cancer J Clin editorial on ACROSS2 and early intensification for EGFR-mutant NSCLC with tumour suppressor co-mutations; abstract missing, commentary on a phase 3 trial. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41818041/
- Clinical and molecular characterization of a large Brazilian lung cancer cohort: a real-world observational study. (Lancet Reg Health Am). Lancet Regional Health Americas cohort of 1131 Brazilian lung cancer patients: EGFR in 20.6%; TP53 co-mutation associated with shorter cancer-specific survival on EGFR inhibitors (24 vs 61 mo); single referral centre, retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41815386/
- Molecular epidemiology and outcomes of EGFR exon 20 insertion in Korean patients with non-small cell lung cancer. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Three-centre Korean cohort (n=2209 EGFR-mutant aNSCLC): exon 20 insertions (2.4%) had median OS 13.9 vs 31.0 months, and PCR missed 47.6% of NGS-detected exon 20 insertions; retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41808707/
- PKC-iota drives EGFR-TKI resistance in EGFR-mutated NSCLC by phosphorylating FASN to reprogram lipid metabolism. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Preclinical mechanistic study (cell and lipidomic assays) proposing PKC-iota phosphorylation of FASN as a driver of EGFR-TKI resistance; mechanistic with a possible therapeutic angle but no clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41808706/
- Constitutive EGFR Activation Induced by PTPRR Downregulation Confers Resistance to KRAS Inhibitors. (Cancer Res Commun). Preclinical study showing PTPRR loss activates EGFR to drive sotorasib resistance in KRAS G12C NSCLC cells; resistance mechanism with a combination rationale, no clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41808249/
- Structure-based classification for differential tyrosine kinase inhibitors efficacy of EGFR exon 19 deletions in non-small cell lung cancer. (Sci China Life Sci). Two-centre retrospective cohort (n=399) plus docking/in vitro work defining a 'CLASS 3G' EGFR 19del subgroup with shorter PFS on third-generation TKIs (12.8 vs 25.6 months) that appeared to benefit from adding chemotherapy; interesting but retrospective with small subgroups. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42144506/
- Update on the management of leptomeningeal disease in solid organ malignancy: Systemic, intrathecal and novel therapies. (J Clin Neurosci). PRISMA systematic review of prospective studies of systemic, intrathecal and novel therapies for leptomeningeal disease in solid tumours including lung; relevant to CNS management but not EGFR-specific. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42140114/
- PD-L1 expression is associated with inferior outcomes independent of EGFR subtype in advanced non-small cell lung cancer with uncommon EGFR mutations treated with first-line second-generation EGFR-tyrosine kinase inhibitors. (Cancer). Dual-centre retrospective real-world study (n=215) of uncommon EGFR mutations on first-line second-generation TKIs finding PD-L1 expression associated with inferior outcomes independent of mutation subtype; retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42136330/
- Real-world treatment and healthcare resource utilization patterns among patients with advanced non-small cell lung cancer treated with amivantamab: a retrospective study using insurance claims data. (BMC Pulm Med). US claims-based real-world study of treatment patterns and resource use among patients starting amivantamab in second line or later; descriptive, no efficacy outcomes. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42129721/
- BLU-451, a CNS-Active, Potent, and Selective Small-Molecule Inhibitor Against Uncommon EGFR Mutations. (Mol Cancer Ther). Preclinical characterisation of BLU-451, a CNS-active selective TKI against uncommon EGFR mutations and exon 20 insertions, with xenograft activity; new agent but no clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42126003/
- Upfront management of brain metastases in oncogene‑addicted NSCLC - An EORTC survey. (Lung Cancer). EORTC/ESTRO practice survey of upfront management of brain metastases across 10 oncogene drivers including EGFR; useful view of practice variation but survey data only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42114373/
- Neoadjuvant Chemoimmunotherapy Followed by Surgery for Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: Current Evidence and Unmet Needs. (Eur J Cardiothorac Surg). Narrative review of neoadjuvant chemoimmunotherapy trials (CheckMate 816, KEYNOTE-671, AEGEAN, CheckMate 77T) in resectable NSCLC; no new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42102248/
- Evolution of bispecific and multispecific antibodies in cancer therapy. (Lancet Reg Health Eur). Lancet Regional Health Europe Series review of bispecific and multispecific antibodies, including in EGFR-mutant NSCLC; overview without new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42099877/
- GLUT6-facilitated noncanonical glucose metabolic rewiring enables resistance to targeted cancer therapy. (Nat Commun). Nature Communications preclinical study identifying a MAZ-GLUT6-NRF2 axis that drives resistance to EGFR and KRAS inhibitors, with targeting GLUT6 overcoming resistance in models; mechanistic with a therapeutic angle but no clinical intervention data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42098139/
- OB-001 Enhances Osimertinib Brain Penetration: Preclinical Pharmacokinetics and Translational Rationale for EGFR-Mutant NSCLC with CNS Disease. (Cancer Res Commun). Preclinical mouse PK study showing an elacridar formulation (OB-001) increases osimertinib brain penetration; CNS-efficacy angle but no clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42096692/
- EGFR-19del nuclear translocation increases HDAC7 expression inhibiting the Hippo pathway and exacerbating TKI resistance in lung adenocarcinoma. (Theranostics). Preclinical study linking nuclear EGFR-19del translocation to HDAC7 upregulation and Hippo pathway inhibition in TKI resistance, with in vivo work; mechanistic with a possible therapeutic angle. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42094614/
- Survival, Toxicity, and Economic Outcomes of Osimertinib Versus Second-Generation Epidermal Growth Factor Receptor-Tyrosine Kinase Inhibitors in Metastatic Epidermal Growth Factor Receptor-Mutant Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (JCO Oncol Pract). Target-trial emulation in TriNetX (777 vs 777 matched) finding longer OS with first-line osimertinib than second-generation TKIs (53.4 vs 33.2 months, HR 0.618) and lower healthcare use; large real-world cohort with residual confounding. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42090641/
- Potential of Minimal Residual Disease in Guiding Adjuvant Therapy Decisions in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (JCO Precis Oncol). Prospective cohort (n=165) showing landmark ctDNA MRD after resection strongly predicts DFS (HR 12.0) and exploring MRD-guided adjuvant decisions including in EGFR-mutant patients; observational with small subgroups. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42090629/
- Hypoxic niche drives lineage imbalance and early tumorigenesis in EGFR-mutant lung cancer. (Am J Respir Crit Care Med). Multi-omics mechanistic study linking hypoxic niche to aggressive early progression of EGFR-mutant LUAD; mechanistic, limited direct treatment implications. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42085227/
- Risk of peripheral edema in cancer patients receiving MET inhibitors: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (J Chemother). Meta-analysis of 35 RCTs (n=10,555) showing MET inhibitors including amivantamab raise peripheral edema risk (all-grade RR 2.76); well-done safety meta-analysis relevant to EGFR-MET therapy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42070585/
- Real-World Treatment Patterns and Clinical Outcomes Among Chinese Patients with Stage III NSCLC: The MOOREA Study. (Target Oncol). Prospective multicentre Chinese real-world study (MOOREA, 28 hospitals) of testing, treatment patterns and outcomes in stage III NSCLC; descriptive real-world data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42070217/
- Clinical impact of local consolidative therapy in EGFR-mutant metastatic NSCLC: A propensity-matched multicenter analysis. (Lung Cancer). Multicentre retrospective propensity-matched cohort (n=864) in which consolidative surgery with first-line EGFR-TKI was associated with longer OS (HR 0.36) and PFS, with less consistent benefit for RT; large but subject to selection bias. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42068892/
- Radiation therapy plus osimertinib vs osimertinib alone in epidermal growth factor receptor mutated non-small cell lung cancer: A systematic review. (World J Methodol). Systematic review of radiotherapy plus osimertinib vs osimertinib alone for EGFR-mutant brain metastases; relevant CNS question, evidence mostly non-randomised. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42058805/
- A real-world study on the comparative effectiveness of first-line treatment regimens in advanced NSCLC patients with PACC mutations. (Ther Adv Med Oncol). Retrospective cohort (n=100) of PACC-type atypical EGFR mutations in which first-line TKIs gave longer PFS than chemotherapy (14.2 vs 5.2 months, HR 0.348), with second/third-generation TKIs appearing better than first-generation; small subgroups and retrospective design. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42046730/
- ABTB1 modulates EGFR signaling and confers icotinib sensitivity in lung adenocarcinoma. (Respir Res). CRISPR screen plus RNA-seq identifying ABTB1 as a modulator of EGFR signalling and icotinib sensitivity in LUAD models; mechanistic, no clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42046060/
- Sphingosine-1-Phosphate Receptor 3 Confers Tumor Metastasis in Lung Cancer Resistant to Third-Generation EGFR Inhibitor. (MedComm (2020)). Preclinical study implicating S1PR3 in metastasis after resistance to third-generation EGFR TKIs (osimertinib, limertinib) in tumour models; mechanistic with a potential target. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42039707/
- A plain language review of results from the LAURA study: osimertinib after chemoradiotherapy for patients with EGFR-mutated non-small cell lung cancer that cannot be removed by surgery. (Future Oncol). Plain-language summary of LAURA (osimertinib after chemoradiotherapy in unresectable stage III EGFR-mutant NSCLC); abstract missing and no new data, but covers a key phase 3 trial. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42037107/
- Perioperative strategies for resectable EGFR-Mutant NSCLC: evidence hierarchy and clinical decision-making. (Cancer Treat Rev). Cancer Treatment Reviews review of perioperative strategies for resectable EGFR-mutant NSCLC framed by evidence maturity (ADAURA etc.); review without new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42033967/
- Discovery and development of the preclinical candidate (SH-1092), a potent third generation EGFR inhibitor for the treatment of NSCLC. (Bioorg Chem). Medicinal chemistry discovery of SH-1092, a third-generation EGFR inhibitor preclinical candidate active against T790M/L858R; early preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42030648/
- STAT3 signaling mediates EGFR-TKI resistance in non-small cell lung cancer by regulating stemness markers and telomerase, reversed by icaritin. (Br J Cancer). Preclinical study showing STAT3 signalling mediates EGFR-TKI resistance via stemness and telomerase, reversed by icaritin in vitro and in mice, with IHC in pre/post-TKI samples; mechanistic. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42020777/
- Evaluating Effective Biomarker Testing for Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer in US Community Oncology Practices. (JCO Oncol Pract). Retrospective study across 30 US community practices of effective biomarker testing before first-line treatment in advanced NSCLC; relevant to access to EGFR-directed therapy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42018972/
- Treatment Patterns Across Lines of Therapy for Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer in the United States. (Cancer Med). Flatiron real-world cohort (n=13,369; 2,283 EGFR-mutant) describing treatment patterns and discontinuation across lines of therapy, noting many patients with drivers did not receive the matching TKI; descriptive. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42007970/
- Mutation-Specific Response to Ramucirumab in EGFR-Mutated Metastatic NSCLC: Insights From Circulating Cell-Free DNA Profiling (Liquid Biopsy Japan Addendum of RELAY Phase 3 Randomized Study). (JTO Clin Res Rep). Exploratory cfDNA analysis (n=131) from the Japanese liquid-biopsy addendum of phase 3 RELAY showing differing cfDNA dynamics and T790M rates by ex19del vs L858R with ramucirumab plus erlotinib; post hoc and small subgroups. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42006277/
- Efficacy of Adding Immune Checkpoint Inhibition to Chemotherapy, With or Without VEGF Inhibition, in Patients With Advanced EGFR-Mutated NSCLC: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis With Reconstructed Individual Patient Data. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Meta-analysis with reconstructed IPD of 7 RCTs (n=2,196) after EGFR-TKI failure: chemo-immunotherapy plus VEGF inhibition improved PFS (HR 0.59) and OS (HR 0.77) vs chemotherapy, with modest benefit without VEGFi; well-done meta-analysis of a key post-TKI question. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42005789/
- PDK1 elevation was induced by epigenetic modifications of KDM3A and METTL16 to mediate TKI resistance and cancer development. (Genes Dis). Preclinical study showing PDK1 upregulation via KDM3A/METTL16 epigenetic regulation mediates gefitinib and osimertinib resistance in cell lines; mechanistic. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42004224/
- Serial Plasma Comprehensive Genomic Profiling Captures Therapy Resistance and Guides Management of Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Cancer Res Commun). Retrospective single-institution study (n=718; 214 EGFR-mutant) showing serial plasma genomic profiling identified actionable resistance alterations in 22% of EGFR-mutant patients after TKI progression, 58% of whom were matched to targeted therapy; retrospective, single centre. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42001597/
- ELMO2 is a therapeutic vulnerability in mesenchymal-like and drug-resistant non-small cell lung cancer. (Nat Commun). Nature Communications preclinical study identifying ELMO2 as a synthetic-lethal vulnerability in mesenchymal-like and drug-resistant NSCLC including EMT-associated EGFR-TKI resistance; mechanistic with a potential target. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41997974/
- Altered Sphingolipid Metabolism is Associated with Osimertinib Resistance in Nonsmall-Cell Lung Cancer. (J Proteome Res). Lipidomic study of osimertinib-resistant cell models linking altered sphingolipid metabolism to resistance; mechanistic, preclinical only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41988688/
- Therapy-induced cholesterol biosynthesis drives lung cancer dormancy and drug resistance. (J Clin Invest). JCI preclinical study showing targeted therapy (including EGFR TKIs) induces cholesterol biosynthesis driving dormancy, with statins plus aurora kinase inhibition nearly eradicating tumour cells in models; mechanistic with treatment implications, no clinical data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41983392/
- Real-world cardiovascular risk comparison of first-line osimertinib and earlier generation EGFR-TKIs in EGFR-mutated NSCLC: a TriNetX USA network analysis. (BMC Cancer). TriNetX propensity-matched cohort (358 pairs) finding first-line osimertinib did not raise overall 5-year cardiovascular risk vs earlier TKIs (HR 0.951), though heart failure risk was higher under age 60; real-world safety data with residual confounding. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41981516/
- The splicing factor hnRNPA1 promotes osimertinib resistance in lung adenocarcinoma by regulating NEDD4L alternative splicing. (Oncogene). Oncogene preclinical study showing hnRNPA1 promotes osimertinib resistance via NEDD4L alternative splicing, with CDX models; mechanistic. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42321531/
- Safety evaluation of epidermal growth factor receptor tyrosine kinase inhibitors in patients with interstitial pneumonia: a study using a Japanese nationwide database. (Future Oncol). Japanese nationwide claims study (n=13,092) finding pre-existing interstitial pneumonia roughly doubled severe drug-induced ILD risk on first-line EGFR-TKIs (SHR 1.85); large real-world safety cohort. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42305076/
- Neoadjuvant retlirafusp alfa (anti-PD-L1/TGF-β bifunctional fusion protein) with or without chemotherapy in unresectable stage III non-small cell lung cancer: updated results from the phase 2 TRAILBLAZER trial. (Signal Transduct Target Ther). Updated phase 2 TRAILBLAZER results of neoadjuvant retlirafusp alfa with or without chemotherapy in unresectable stage III EGFR/ALK-negative NSCLC (3-year EFS 50.5% in arms A+B); phase 2 with small randomised arms. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42303632/
- Duration of systemic therapy in patients with metastatic EGFR-mutated non-small cell lung cancer and brain metastases. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Population-based Ontario cohort (481 patients with brain metastases) in which first-line osimertinib vs gefitinib was associated with longer treatment duration (12.6 vs 6.4 months), CNS PFS (21.7 vs 13.5 months) and OS (24.7 vs 13.6 months); real-world with surrogate definition of brain metastases. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42291352/
- Impact of prophylactic skin toxicity management on treatment continuity and clinical outcomes in EGFR-mutant non-small cell lung cancer treated with osimertinib: a 7-year real-world retrospective cohort study. (Support Care Cancer). Single-centre retrospective cohort (n=120) in which proactive skin care during osimertinib was associated with less severe skin toxicity (adjusted OR 0.122) and fewer treatment interruptions; relevant to tolerance but retrospective and small. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42287425/
- Real-world testing practices, treatment patterns, and outcomes of tyrosine kinase inhibitors (TKIs) in epidermal growth factor receptor-mutated advanced/metastatic non-small cell lung cancer: An international study. (Cancer Treat Res Commun). International retrospective chart review (947 patients, 9 countries) of EGFR testing practices and TKI use, showing mostly single-gene testing and variable first-line TKI use; descriptive data on access differences between countries. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42284848/
- ResMap: A community resource for systematic mapping of therapy-persistent residual cancer cell dependencies across contexts. (Sci Adv). Science Advances community resource (ResMap) systematically testing 94 compounds against drug-tolerant persister cells in EGFR-mutant and KRAS G12C lung cancer models; preclinical resource. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42284402/
- Surgical Removal of Residual Tumor Masses in Patients Undergoing Targeted Therapy for EGFR-Mutated Locally Advanced or Metastatic Lung Cancer. (Int J Cancer). Retrospective study (n=230) in which cytoreductive surgery for residual disease during first-line EGFR-TKI response (n=41) was associated with longer PFS (28.4 vs 15.4 months) and OS vs non-operated resectable patients; selection bias limits interpretation. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42276818/
- Adjuvant chemotherapy for resected stage-ia lung adenocarcinoma with micropapillary histologic pattern. (Interdiscip Cardiovasc Thorac Surg). Phase II trial with concurrent non-randomised comparison (81 vs 75) of adjuvant pemetrexed-carboplatin in resected stage IA micropapillary LUAD showing no RFS difference (5-year 88.9% vs 86.7%); adjuvant data but small and non-randomised. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42266006/
- Furmonertinib-based triplet therapy improves survival in EGFR-mutant NSCLC with leptomeningeal metastases: a large-scale multicenter retrospective study (FURMO-LM). (ESMO Open). Large multicentre retrospective study (n=618) of furmonertinib-based regimens in EGFR-mutant NSCLC with leptomeningeal metastases: median OS 14.2 months, longer with triplet furmonertinib plus antiangiogenic plus chemotherapy (17.9 months); CNS-relevant but retrospective and non-randomised. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42247790/
- Pharmacokinetic Acceleration via CYP3A4 Hyperactivation as a Clinically Actionable Mechanism of Targeted Therapy Resistance in NSCLC. (bioRxiv). Preprint, not peer reviewed. Preprint (bioRxiv, not peer reviewed) identifying hepatic CYP3A4 hyperactivation as a tumour-extrinsic mechanism of resistance to EGFR, ALK and KRAS G12C inhibitors in mouse models, with retrospective clinical correlation; potentially actionable through dosing. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42239162/
- Role of VEGFA/VEGFR2 signaling in predicting clinical outcomes of EGFR-TKI treatment in EGFR-mutant non-small cell lung cancer. (Br J Cancer). Single-centre retrospective study (447 early-stage, 146 relapsed, 60 advanced) finding high VEGFA associated with shorter PFS on first/second-generation TKIs but not osimertinib; biomarker with possible treatment-selection relevance. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42231027/
- Cardiovascular adverse events in non-small cell lung cancer patients receiving osimertinib therapy: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Lung Cancer). Systematic review and meta-analysis (61 studies, n=10,316) of cardiovascular adverse events on osimertinib, with hypertension most common and higher QTc/LVEF events with osimertinib plus platinum-pemetrexed; mostly non-randomised sources. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42229338/
- Afatinib Versus Osimertinib for Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer With Uncommon EGFR Mutations: Real-World Outcomes. (Cancer Sci). Multicentre Japanese retrospective study (29 hospitals, n=162) of first-line afatinib vs osimertinib in uncommon EGFR mutations showing similar TTF (HR 1.04) and OS, with subtype-specific differences; retrospective IPTW analysis. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42218657/
- Amivantamab in advanced non-small cell lung cancer with epidermal growth factor receptor exon 20 insertion mutations: Real-world data from the Italian ATLAS Registry. (Cancer). Italian ATLAS registry (n=119; 64 received amivantamab) of exon 20 insertion NSCLC: ORR 37.5%, median PFS 9.6 months, OS 16.9 months, grade 3-4 TRAEs 10.9%; real-world confirmation of CHRYSALIS, retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42216441/
- Initial biomarker testing strategies and clinical outcomes in advanced non-small cell lung cancer. (iScience). Multicentre retrospective comparison of single-plex vs multiplex initial testing showing higher EGFR detection, more EGFR-TKI use and longer OS with single-plex; relevant to access to EGFR therapy but retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42205705/
- The Role of PD-L1 in Treatment Decision-Making for Perioperative EGFR-Mutated Lung Cancer and its Genomic Analysis. (Ann Surg Oncol). Surgical cohort with genomic analysis suggesting PD-L1 >=1% marks higher recurrence risk and better immunochemotherapy response in perioperative EGFR-mutant NSCLC; retrospective, needs prospective validation. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42201492/
- Comparison of Post-Resistance Treatment Outcomes in EGFR-Mutated NSCLC Patients Following Third-Generation EGFR-TKI Therapy: A Retrospective Cohort Study. (Technol Cancer Res Treat). Single-centre retrospective cohort (n=206) of treatments after third-generation EGFR-TKI resistance favouring chemo-antiangiogenesis over chemo-immunotherapy; retrospective, prospective validation needed. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42200358/
- Amivantamab plus chemotherapy vs. chemotherapy as first-line treatment in Chinese mainland patients with EGFR exon 20 insertion non-small cell lung cancer: Subgroup analysis of the randomized PAPILLON trial. (Chin Med J (Engl)). Chinese mainland subgroup (n=87) of the phase 3 PAPILLON trial showing PFS HR 0.47 for amivantamab plus chemotherapy in EGFR exon 20 insertion NSCLC; consistent with the main trial, small regional subgroup. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42192224/
- Optimal treatment strategies for EGFR mutant advanced lung adenocarcinoma patients with targeted therapy resistance and correlation analysis of PD-L1 expression with ICI efficacy. (Sci Rep). Retrospective cohort (n=107) after first-line third-generation TKI failure suggesting PFS benefit of adding ICIs in PD-L1-positive patients; retrospective with small subgroups. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42185471/
- Can local consolidation therapy improve survival in EGFR-positive advanced non-small cell lung cancer after first-line TKI treatment?-a systematic review and meta-analysis. (J Thorac Dis). Systematic review and meta-analysis finding that local consolidation therapy after first-line TKI improves OS and PFS in EGFR-mutant advanced NSCLC without more toxicity; mixed underlying study designs. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42182703/
- Efficacy and safety of first-line osimertinib monotherapy versus osimertinib plus platinum-based chemotherapy or amivantamab plus lazertinib in metastatic EGFR-mutated NSCLC: an indirect comparison. (Front Oncol). Indirect comparison of FLAURA2-type osimertinib plus chemotherapy vs amivantamab plus lazertinib vs osimertinib suggesting similar survival benefit with different toxicity; indirect, no head-to-head data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42180117/
- Double-dose furmonertinib for patients with osimertinib-resistant advanced NSCLC: therapeutic efficacy and safety. (Front Oncol). Retrospective study (n=69) of double-dose furmonertinib after osimertinib resistance showing higher DCR and mPFS 4.97 vs 3.20 months vs chemotherapy; small, non-randomised. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42180046/
- ARID1A loss drives osimertinib resistance by epigenetically suppressing PTEN and activating the Akt pathway in lung cancer. (Biochem Biophys Res Commun). Preclinical mechanistic study showing ARID1A loss drives osimertinib resistance via PTEN suppression and Akt activation; in vitro, clinical path unclear. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42172903/
- Study on NGS-based molecular profiling of malignant pleural effusion supernatant cfDNA in guiding targeted therapy for non-small cell lung cancer. (Lung Cancer). Study showing malignant pleural effusion supernatant cfDNA NGS outperforms cell blocks and plasma for detecting actionable mutations; practical testing relevance, single cohort. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42172737/
- Clinicogenomic Landscape of MTAP-Deleted Thoracic Malignancies Across US and Japanese Nationwide Cohorts: Impact of Concurrent CDKN2A Alterations and Driver Mutations. (JCO Precis Oncol). US and Japanese nationwide clinicogenomic cohorts characterising MTAP-deleted thoracic tumours with driver co-alterations; supports MTAP-targeted combinations, descriptive. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42172559/
- Efficacy and safety of first-line osimertinib in Chinese patients with EGFR-mutated advanced non-small cell lung cancer: a prospective, multicenter, non-interventional study (FLOURISH). (Cancer Biol Med). Prospective multicentre non-interventional study (FLOURISH, n=481) of first-line osimertinib in China: median rwPFS 19.4 and OS 41.0 months, worse with co-mutations; large real-world cohort without comparator. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42170792/
- TROP2 expression as a prognostic predictor for osimertinib in patients with EGFR-mutant non-small cell lung cancer. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Retrospective cohort (n=155) showing TROP2 overexpression independently predicts shorter OS on first-line osimertinib; relevant to TROP2-ADC sequencing but single biomarker study. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42170286/
- Estimated Cost of Adverse Event Management in Treatment Naïve Non-small Cell Lung Cancer Patients with Common EGFR Mutations Treated with Amivantamab Plus Lazertinib and Osimertinib Plus Chemotherapy. (J Health Econ Outcomes Res). Cost model of adverse-event management for amivantamab plus lazertinib vs osimertinib plus chemotherapy in first-line EGFR-mutant NSCLC; descriptive cross-trial analysis relevant to access. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42170243/
- Stimulator of interferon genes agonist augmented antitumor immunity of osimertinib in Egfr-mutated lung cancer. (Mol Oncol). Mouse study showing a STING agonist augments osimertinib antitumour immunity with abscopal effect in Egfr-mutant lung cancer; preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42169500/
- Development of the Weight-Tiered Q3W Regimen of Amivantamab in Combination With Carboplatin-Pemetrexed in EGFR-Mutated Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (CPT Pharmacometrics Syst Pharmacol). Population PK and exposure-response analysis supporting the registered weight-tiered Q3W amivantamab plus chemotherapy regimen; relevant to labelling, modelling study. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42166539/
- EGFR-TKI monotherapy versus combination therapy as first-line treatment for EGFR-mutant NSCLC with brain metastases: a real-world comparison analysis. (BMC Cancer). Retrospective cohort (n=150) of EGFR-mutant NSCLC with brain metastases where TKI plus radiotherapy, bevacizumab or chemotherapy improved iPFS and PFS over TKI alone; retrospective with small arms. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42163236/
- A Non-Canonical Core Transcriptional Regulatory Circuit Orchestrates Chromatin Reprogramming to Drive Osimertinib Resistance in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Adv Sci (Weinh)). Preclinical epigenomic study identifying an ID3-SMAD3-NR2F2 circuit upregulating HIF2A that drives osimertinib resistance with neuroendocrine features, reversible by HIF2A inhibition in models; preclinical only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42160004/
- Circulating epigenetic and epitranscriptomic biomarkers identify EGFR status, predict TKI response and reveal therapeutic vulnerabilities in NSCLC. (Sci Rep). Study of circulating epigenetic/epitranscriptomic marks to identify EGFR status and predict TKI response, with preclinical epigenetic combination data; exploratory. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42477403/
- Clinical Utility of Next Generation Sequencing in Concurrent EBUS-TBNA and Liquid Biopsies in NSCLC. (Am J Respir Crit Care Med). Prospective comparison of concurrent EBUS-TBNA tissue and liquid biopsy NGS in NSCLC (AJRCCM) showing tissue detects more mutations but blood is complementary; testing strategy, not EGFR-specific. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42475517/
- AXL phosphosite mapping links FAK1 and YAP1 signaling to erlotinib resistance in EGFR-mutant lung cancer. (Cell Rep). Phosphoproteomic study linking AXL-FAK1-YAP1 signalling to erlotinib tolerance in EGFR-mutant lung cancer; mechanistic, earlier-generation TKI. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42475177/
- Efficacy and safety of intraventricular pemetrexed via Ommaya reservoir in the treatment of leptomeningeal metastasis from EGFR-mutant lung adenocarcinoma who progresses on third-generation TKIs: A single-arm clinical trial. (Chin Med J (Engl)). Single-arm trial of intraventricular pemetrexed via Ommaya reservoir for leptomeningeal metastasis after third-generation TKI progression; abstract missing, so results cannot be assessed. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42473043/
- Cost-Effectiveness of Combination Amivantamab-Lazertinib, Osimertinib Plus Chemotherapy, and Osimertinib Monotherapy in Lung Cancer. (J Natl Compr Canc Netw). Cost-effectiveness model (Taiwan and US) finding neither amivantamab-lazertinib nor osimertinib plus chemotherapy cost-effective vs osimertinib monotherapy; access-relevant modelling. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42462790/
- Adjuvant chemotherapy with or without EGFR-TKIs for resected EGFR-mutated non-small cell lung cancer: a pooled analysis of four randomized controlled trials. (BMC Cancer). Meta-analysis of five RCTs (n=890) finding adjuvant EGFR-TKI plus chemotherapy improved OS (HR 0.56) and DFS vs chemotherapy alone in resected EGFR-mutant NSCLC; mostly earlier-generation TKI trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42458364/
- Coexisting driver alterations in non-small cell lung cancer: Prevalence, clonal architecture, and impact on survival and progression. (Eur J Cancer). Large cohort (n=5420) characterising coexisting driver mutations in NSCLC and their prognostic impact; descriptive genomic real-world data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42456541/
- Concurrent chemoradiotherapy with or without simultaneous integrated boost for unresectable stage III non-small-cell lung cancer: a randomized phase II trial with exploratory EGFR and PD-L1 analyses. (Cancer Genet). Single-centre randomised phase II trial (n=168, enrolled 2015-2018) where simultaneous integrated boost chemoradiotherapy improved PFS (HR 0.48) and OS (HR 0.55) in stage III NSCLC, with exploratory EGFR/PD-L1 analyses; pre-consolidation-immunotherapy era. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42456525/
- EMBER-Lung: Electronic Medical Record Boosting Molecular Testing in Early-Stage Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (JCO Precis Oncol). Prospective study showing an EMR nudge after resection increased comprehensive molecular testing and EGFR detection in early-stage non-squamous NSCLC; relevant to access to adjuvant targeted therapy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42456087/
- Relationships Between Clinician-Confirmed Symptomatic Progression and Change in Patient-Reported Outcomes in Non-Small-Cell Lung Cancer. (Value Health). Analysis linking clinician-confirmed symptomatic progression with worsening PROs in NSCLC trials, supporting this endpoint; methodological. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42447989/
- First-line third-generation EGFR-TKIs for advanced EGFR-mutated non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and network meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials. (J Thorac Dis). Network meta-analysis of RCTs of first-line third-generation EGFR-TKIs finding similar efficacy across agents and superior PFS to first-generation TKIs; indirect comparisons. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42444976/
- Perioperative immune checkpoint inhibitors with or without chemotherapy versus placebo with or without chemotherapy in elderly people with localised non-small cell lung cancer. (Cochrane Database Syst Rev). Cochrane systematic review of perioperative ICIs in older adults with resectable NSCLC; results not shown in the truncated abstract. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42440312/
- Mesenchymal stem cell-induced metabolic reprogramming of EGFR-wild-type tumor cells drives therapeutic resistance in EGFR-mutant non-small cell lung cancer. (J Exp Clin Cancer Res). Mechanistic study showing tumour-associated MSCs reprogram EGFR-wild-type tumour cell metabolism to drive TKI resistance in EGFR-mutant NSCLC, with spatial patient-sample data; preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42436555/
- Optimal treatment strategies for unresectable stage III EGFR-mutated non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and Bayesian network meta-analysis. (Front Oncol). Bayesian network meta-analysis (12 studies, n=1,529) in unresectable stage III EGFR-mutant NSCLC favouring CRT plus EGFR-TKI for OS (HR 0.63 vs CRT); mixes RCTs with retrospective studies. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42434745/
- Prognostic analysis and beneficiary population exploration of subsequent treatment regimens after third-generation EGFR-TKIs failure in EGFR-mutated advanced non-small cell lung cancer: a retrospective cohort study. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Single-centre retrospective cohort (n=225) after third-generation TKI failure where ICI-containing regimens, especially ICI plus bevacizumab plus chemotherapy, gave longer PFS but not OS; retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42433249/
- Oncogenic EGFR rewires STING-TBK1 signalosomes to license DNA damage tolerance in NSCLC. (EMBO J). EMBO J mechanistic study showing mutant EGFR rewires STING-TBK1 to enable DNA damage tolerance, with TBK1 inhibition plus cisplatin active in TKI-resistant organoids and mice; preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42432254/
- Design and development of novel EGFR inhibitors spanning all subgroups of EGFR mutations in non-small cell lung cancer. (Innovation (Camb)). Structure-based design of pan-EGFR inhibitor ZW-49 active against classical, exon 20 insertion and PACC mutations with xenograft regression, now in phase 1; preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42422019/
- USP20-mediated PGAM1 stabilization promotes glycolysis and confers osimertinib resistance in non-small cell lung carcinoma. (Oncogene). Oncogene study showing USP20-PGAM1-driven glycolysis confers osimertinib resistance, reversed by a USP20 inhibitor in functional assays; preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42420587/
- EGFR Mutations and Recurrence Outcomes in Resected Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: An Updated 2015-2025 Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (Thorac Cancer). Systematic review and meta-analysis (2015-2025) finding EGFR status not prognostic for recurrence after resection while adjuvant EGFR-TKIs improved DFS (HR 0.45); mixes study designs. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42414222/
- Resection of Primary Lung Tumors in Patients with Stage IV EGFR-Mutant Lung Adenocarcinoma before Progression under First-Line TKI Treatment and Its Impact on Prognosis. (Ann Surg Oncol). Single-centre propensity-matched cohort (26 vs 78) suggesting primary tumour resection before progression on first-line TKI improves PFS and OS in stage IV EGFR-mutant LUAD; small, selection-biased. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42412352/
- CD39+CD8+ T cell infiltration is associated with clinical outcomes of subsequent immunotherapy in advanced EGFR-mutant non-small cell lung cancer. (Ther Adv Med Oncol). Exploratory study linking CD39+CD8+ T-cell infiltration to PFS on subsequent immunotherapy in EGFR-mutant NSCLC; small biomarker study. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42403504/
- Inhibition of ferroptosis via SLC25A39-NRF2 axis drives Osimertinib resistance in lung adenocarcinoma. (Cell Mol Life Sci). Preclinical study showing SLC25A39-NRF2 ferroptosis suppression drives osimertinib resistance, with ferroptosis induction enhancing osimertinib in vivo; preclinical. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42402515/
- Osimertinib-induced cardiotoxicity is driven by HDAC-dependent epigenetic repression and rescued by vorinostat. (Signal Transduct Target Ther). Preclinical study showing osimertinib cardiotoxicity is HDAC-dependent and rescued by vorinostat in mice; preclinical safety mechanism. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42386709/
- Adjuvant targeted therapy in high-risk stage IB epidermal growth factor receptor-mutant lung cancer: Role of ground-glass opacity components. (JTCVS Open). Propensity-matched retrospective cohort (n=361) finding adjuvant EGFR-TKI did not improve DFS or OS overall in high-risk stage IB EGFR-mutant NSCLC, with DFS benefit only in pure-solid tumours; only 41 treated. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42381941/
- An Observational Study of Afatinib 30 mg Daily in Patients With Advanced Non-Small-Cell Lung Cancer Harboring Common EGFR Mutations Treated With Afatinib. (Thorac Cancer). Single-arm phase 2 of afatinib 30 mg first line (n=30, terminated early) with 6-month PFS 93.2% and fewer grade 3 AEs; small, underpowered. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42381340/
- Association Between EGFR-TKI-Associated Skin Rash and Recorded Mortality in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Real-World Analysis Accounting for Immortal Time Bias. (Pharmacoepidemiol Drug Saf). Real-world pharmacoepidemiology analysis showing EGFR-TKI skin rash is associated with lower mortality (HR 0.62) but less so after correcting for immortal time bias; methodological correction to prior literature. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42381293/
- Atezolizumab and bevacizumab plus chemotherapy versus chemotherapy in non-small cell lung cancer after tyrosine kinase inhibitor failure. (Ther Adv Med Oncol). Multicentre retrospective study (n=114) after TKI failure in EGFR/ALK/ROS1 NSCLC where atezolizumab-bevacizumab-carboplatin-paclitaxel improved PFS (7.3 vs 3.0 months) but not OS vs pemetrexed-platinum; retrospective, mixed drivers. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42369728/
- Clinical characteristics and outcomes of advanced EGFR-mutated NSCLC treated with 45 or 30 mg starting doses of dacomitinib: a retrospective multicenter analysis. (Ther Adv Med Oncol). Multicentre retrospective comparison suggesting first-line dacomitinib 30 mg gives similar efficacy with better tolerability than 45 mg; retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42369726/
- Cost-effectiveness of neoadjuvant and adjuvant novel cancer therapies in stage II-III non-small cell lung cancer in Sri Lanka. (BMJ Oncol). Sri Lanka cost-effectiveness modelling of perioperative novel therapies including TKIs for stage II-III NSCLC; access-relevant, country-specific. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42369562/
- Reassessing the Incidence and Risk Factors of Radiation Pneumonitis in Treatment-Naive EGFR-Mutant NSCLC With Concurrent Third-Generation EGFR-TKI and Thoracic Radiotherapy. (Int J Cancer). Single-centre retrospective study (n=209) of radiation pneumonitis with first-line third-generation EGFR-TKI plus thoracic radiotherapy, identifying dosimetric risk factors; needs external validation. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42363769/
- Spatial proteomics profiling reveals oncogene-specific immune niches and prognostic markers in NSCLC. (Am J Respir Crit Care Med). AJRCCM spatial proteomics study showing EGFR- and KRAS-mutant NSCLC create distinct immunosuppressive niches with prognostic spatial metrics; mechanistic, exploratory. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42363408/
- Association of TP53 gain-of-function mutations with early osimertinib resistance in epidermal growth factor receptor-mutant lung adenocarcinoma. (ESMO Open). Genomic cohort (n=140) on first-line osimertinib showing TP53 gain-of-function mutations predict much shorter PFS (12.0 vs 31.4 months vs wild type); retrospective, single country. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42361448/
- Survival outcomes and a prognostic nomogram in EGFR-mutant non-small cell lung cancer with leptomeningeal metastasis: a real-world cohort study. (J Neurooncol). Retrospective cohort (n=288) of EGFR-mutant NSCLC with leptomeningeal metastasis linking post-LM third-generation TKI to longer survival, with an internally validated nomogram. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42360664/
- Early Detriment Analysis of First-Line Nivolumab plus Ipilimumab-Based Therapy in Patients with Metastatic Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Clin Cancer Res). Exploratory analysis of phase III CheckMate 227/9LA identifying factors associated with early progression on nivolumab-ipilimumab in EGFR/ALK-negative NSCLC; post hoc. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42360104/
- Real-World Outcomes and Progression Patterns with First-Line Osimertinib in Patients with Advanced EGFR-Mutant Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Nationwide Turkish Oncology Group Study. (Cancers (Basel)). Nationwide retrospective Turkish cohort (n=143, 33 centres) of first-line osimertinib: median PFS 17.6 months, half of progressions oligoprogressive, with local ablative therapy linked to longer osimertinib continuation; retrospective, moderate size. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42352512/
- Real-world prevalence of actionable genomic alterations detected by next-generation sequencing in non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Clin Transl Oncol). Systematic review and meta-analysis of real-world prevalence of actionable alterations detected by NGS in NSCLC; informs testing pathways. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42348103/
- Antibody-based regimens targeting PD-1/PD-L1 and VEGF/VEGFR in advanced or metastatic NSCLC: a meta-analysis of RCTs. (Front Immunol). Meta-analysis of RCTs of PD-(L)1 plus VEGF-targeting antibody regimens in advanced NSCLC, with subgroup HR 0.80 in EGFR-mutant patients; subgroup-level pooled data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42344916/
- Sex disparities in lung cancer: Results of the KBP-2020-CPHG study. (Lung Cancer). French national KBP-2020-CPHG observational study of sex differences in lung cancer outcomes; large cohort, not EGFR-specific. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42341535/
- VEGF-A blockade overcomes liver metastases resistance to chemoimmunotherapy in patients with advanced non-squamous NSCLC. (J Immunother Cancer). Post hoc analysis of phase III IMpower130/150 (n=1,713, EGFR/ALK wild type) suggesting bevacizumab restores chemoimmunotherapy benefit in liver metastases; hypothesis-generating. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42336655/
- SUNRAY-02: a study of olomorasib combined with immunotherapy in stage II-III KRAS G12C-mutant non-small cell lung cancer after definitive therapy. (Future Oncol). Design paper for the phase 3 SUNRAY-02 trial of adjuvant/consolidation olomorasib plus immunotherapy in stage II-III KRAS G12C NSCLC; protocol, no results. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42823821/
- Safety and Antitumor Activity of Farletuzumab Ecteribulin in Patients With NSCLC: Phase I Expansion Results. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Phase I expansion (n=15) of FRalpha-targeted ADC farletuzumab ecteribulin in pretreated NSCLC with ORR 6.7%; small, low activity, not EGFR-specific. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41993769/
- Transcription Factor Subtype Governs Response and Resistance to DLL3-Directed T-Cell Engagement in Small Cell Lung Cancer. (bioRxiv). Preprint, not peer reviewed. Preprint (not peer reviewed) linking SCLC transcription-factor subtype to response and resistance to DLL3-directed T-cell engagers; relevant to SCLC therapy, unreviewed. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41993528/
- Modeling acquired TKI resistance and effective combination therapeutic strategies in murine RET+ lung adenocarcinoma. (Cancer Lett). Murine RET+ lung adenocarcinoma models of acquired TKI resistance showing upfront selpercatinib plus crizotinib eliminated most tumours; preclinical, non-EGFR driver. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41921856/
- [Management of ocular adverse events associated with antibody-drug conjugates in the treatment of solid tumors: a consensus statement from Chinese experts (2025 edition)]. (Zhonghua Zhong Liu Za Zhi). Chinese expert consensus on managing ocular adverse events of ADCs across solid tumours including NSCLC; safety guidance, not efficacy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41876197/
- [Expert consensus on the clinical application of TROP2-targeted antibody-drug conjugates in non-small cell lung cancer (2026 edition)]. (Zhonghua Zhong Liu Za Zhi). Chinese expert consensus on clinical use and AE management of TROP2 ADCs (sacituzumab tirumotecan, datopotamab deruxtecan, sacituzumab govitecan) in NSCLC; regional consensus summarising existing evidence. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41876196/
- Comparative efficacy of trastuzumab deruxtecan versus guideline-recommended treatments for 2L+ unresectable locally advanced or metastatic HER2-mutant non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and indirect treatment comparison. (Front Oncol). Systematic review and indirect comparison suggesting T-DXd outperforms other approved 2L+ treatments in HER2-mutant NSCLC using external comparator arms; indirect, single-arm source. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41767585/
- Efficacy and safety of tepotinib in MET‑altered non‑small cell lung cancer: a meta-analysis. (Sci Rep). Meta-analysis of six studies (n=546) of tepotinib in MET-altered NSCLC with pooled ORR 52% and median PFS 10.2 months; non-randomised source studies, not post-EGFR specific. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41760894/
- Population Pharmacokinetics and Exposure-Response Analyses for Telisotuzumab Vedotin in Patients With c-Met Protein Overexpressing Tumors. (CPT Pharmacometrics Syst Pharmacol). Population PK and exposure-response analysis for telisotuzumab vedotin, the c-Met ADC with accelerated approval in c-Met-overexpressing NSCLC; supports dosing, modelling study. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41748495/
- Fecal microbiota transplantation for advanced non-small cell lung cancer with secondary PD-1 resistance efficacy prognostic factors and microbiome diversity analysis. (J Transl Med). Single-arm phase II (n=27) of fecal microbiota transplantation plus PD-1 rechallenge and chemotherapy after PD-1 resistance in NSCLC; small, uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41736135/
- TROPION-Lung10: a phase 3 study of datopotamab deruxtecan and rilvegostomig in patients with treatment-naïve locally advanced or metastatic nonsquamous non-small cell lung cancer with high PD-L1 expression and without actionable genomic alterations. (Front Oncol). Design paper for phase 3 TROPION-Lung10 of first-line Dato-DXd plus rilvegostomig vs pembrolizumab in PD-L1-high NSCLC without actionable alterations; protocol, no results. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41669261/
- Global advances and future directions in lung cancer care: expert consensus and strategic priorities. (ESMO Open). ESMO Open expert consensus from the 2025 NY Lung Cancer Foundation summit on global priorities in lung cancer prevention, screening and treatment; broad, not data-driven. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41579430/
- Landscape of Genomic Mechanisms of Resistance to Selective RET Inhibitors in RET-Altered Solid Tumors: Analysis of the RETgistry Global Consortium. (Clin Cancer Res). Global RETgistry consortium (n=109, 143 post-progression biopsies) showing off-target resistance, mainly MET amplification (~18%), after selective RET inhibitors; useful resistance data but outside EGFR and retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41537704/
- SEZ6 expression and lineage plasticity in small cell lung cancer and transformed non-small cell lung cancer. (Lung Cancer). Large RNA-seq dataset (1318 SCLC, 2218 NE) showing high SEZ6 expression in SCLC and in NSCLC-to-SCLC transformed samples, supporting SEZ6-directed ADCs; descriptive, transformation pairs few. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41534317/
- Braf-mutant metastatic non-small-cell lung cancer: Real world data from the Italian biomarker atlas database. (Eur J Cancer). Italian real-world registry of 244 BRAF-mutant NSCLC: first-line dabrafenib/trametinib mPFS 19.8 months, shorter with MET amplification; retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41475048/
- Intravenous lidocaine reduces systemic inflammation but not myocardial injury following thoracic surgery for lung cancer: a randomized controlled trial. (BMC Anesthesiol). Double-blind placebo-controlled RCT of IV lidocaine during lung cancer thoracic surgery: reduced systemic inflammation but no effect on myocardial injury; perioperative but small single-question trial. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41794665/
- Club cell secretory protein (CCSP) serum concentration is a prognostic factor after surgical resection of localized non-small cell lung cancer in patients in the IFCT-0302 trial. (Lung Cancer). Post hoc ancillary analysis of the randomised IFCT-0302 follow-up trial finding postoperative serum CCSP prognostic for OS after NSCLC resection; biomarker, not treatment comparison. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41793820/
- 2000 Peripheral Pulmonary Lesions Sampled by Shape-Sensing Robotic-Assisted Bronchoscopy and Mobile Cone-Beam Computed Tomography: The Mayo Clinic Experience. (Mayo Clin Proc). Multicentre Mayo Clinic series of 2000 peripheral lesions sampled by robotic bronchoscopy with cone-beam CT reporting diagnostic yield; large but retrospective, diagnostic. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41784586/
- The PRECIsE study: a prospective, multicenter study of shape-sensing robotic-assisted bronchoscopy with 2 years of follow-up. (Ann Am Thorac Soc). Prospective multicentre PRECIsE study of robotic bronchoscopy with 2-year follow-up; diagnostic, observational. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41773028/
- A phase 2 study of abemaciclib in patients with brain metastases secondary to non-small cell lung cancer or melanoma. (Neurooncol Adv). Simon two-stage phase II of abemaciclib for brain metastases (NSCLC n=28): no confirmed intracranial responses, mPFS 1.6 months; negative small trial. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41768125/
- Feasibility and preliminary efficacy of an art-making program to manage fear of cancer recurrence (AM-I-FCR) in lung cancer patients: a randomized controlled pilot study. (BMC Med). Pilot RCT (n=60) of an art-making programme reduced fear of recurrence, anxiety and depression in lung cancer patients; single-centre feasibility study with short follow-up. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41761252/
- Efficacy and tolerability of first-line treatment regimens for extensive-stage small cell lung cancer - a grade-assessed network meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials. (BMC Cancer). GRADE-assessed network meta-analysis of RCTs of first-line ES-SCLC regimens; synthesis of existing trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41761164/
- TIGOS Trial: A Randomized, Double-Blind, Phase III Trial of Atigotatug and Nivolumab Fixed-Dose Combination With Chemotherapy Versus Atezolizumab With Chemotherapy as First-Line Therapy in Patients With Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Clin Lung Cancer). Design paper of the TIGOS phase III trial (atigotatug + nivolumab + chemo vs atezolizumab + chemo) in ES-SCLC; protocol of a major trial, no results. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41759251/
- Comparative Risks of Pneumonitis Amongst Immune Checkpoint Inhibitors in Patients with Lung Cancer: A Network Meta-Analysis of Randomized Clinical Trials. (Pharmaceuticals (Basel)). Network meta-analysis of RCTs comparing pneumonitis risk across ICIs in lung cancer; synthesis of existing trial data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41754762/
- Inpatient mortality and failure to rescue in lung cancer patients by hospital certification status: Cross-sectional analysis of nationwide german discharge data 2014-2023. (J Cancer Policy). Nationwide German discharge-data analysis (2014-2023) of in-hospital mortality after lung resection by centre certification; large but administrative and cross-sectional. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41740849/
- Non-intubated video-assisted thoracic surgery for operable lung cancer: a randomized controlled trial. (Int J Surg). Single-centre double-blind RCT (n=120) of non-intubated vs intubated VATS showing lower postoperative inflammatory markers with NIVATS; surrogate endpoints only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41731867/
- Intracranial efficacy of systemic therapies for patients with ALK-positive non-small cell lung cancer in patients with brain metastases: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (J Chemother). Meta-analysis of 8 RCTs (2,250 patients) of first-line ALK TKIs showing better intracranial response with lorlatinib (RR 3.57); synthesis of existing trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41728837/
