- Prognostic significance of circulating tumor DNA as a baseline or dynamic factor in advanced NSCLC without oncogenic drivers: A systematic review and meta-analysis. (Crit Rev Oncol Hematol). PRISMA meta-analysis of ctDNA at baseline and on-treatment as prognostic in driver-negative advanced NSCLC; prognostic, not treatment-guiding. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41724339/
- Efficacy and safety of ivonescimab in non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis of emerging clinical data. (Front Pharmacol). Meta-analysis of 5 studies (1,365 patients) of ivonescimab showing PFS HR 0.53 vs controls with more grade>=3 AEs; small evidence base, no OS. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41710928/
- Fast Real-Time Assessment of Combination Therapies in Immuno-ONcology in patients with advanced non-small-cell lung cancer (FRACTION-Lung). (ESMO Open). Phase II adaptive platform FRACTION-Lung (n=295) of nivolumab combinations: ORRs 0-25% and early discontinuation for limited efficacy; negative, small arms. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41702354/
- Standardized Recommendations for the Implementation of Enhanced Recovery Protocols in Thoracic Surgery in Spain: A Delphi Consensus Study. (Eur J Cardiothorac Surg). Spanish Delphi consensus on ERAS for thoracic surgery (62 recommendations incl. prehabilitation, early mobilisation); national expert consensus without outcome data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41701146/
- World Expert Consensus Recommendations on Shape-Sensing Robotic-Assisted Bronchoscopy for the Diagnosis of Peripheral Pulmonary Lesions. (Respiration). First international expert consensus (18 specialists, 38 statements) on robotic bronchoscopy for peripheral lesions; procedural guidance, expert-opinion based. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41697930/
- Feasibility and safety of nintedanib in combination with nivolumab in pretreated patients with advanced or metastatic adenocarcinoma of the lung - An AIO phase Ib/II trial. (Lung Cancer). Phase Ib/II AIO trial of nintedanib + nivolumab in pretreated lung adenocarcinoma (n=52 at RP2D): 6-month PFS 25%, mOS 12.2 months; single-arm, no synergy overall. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41691771/
- Does the Fissure Last Technique Really Reduce Postoperative Airleak After Lung Resection? Results From a Prospective Randomized Controlled Trial. (Interdiscip Cardiovasc Thorac Surg). Single-centre phase III RCT (n=150) finding fissure-last technique did not reduce air leak after lung resection (50% vs 55%); negative surgical-technique trial. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41689184/
- A Randomized Phase II Trial of Hypo-fractionated Intensity-Modulated Radiation Therapy (IMRT) Utilizing 2.5 Gy/Fraction Versus Standard- Fractionated IMRT, Concurrent With Carboplatin/Paclitaxel and Followed by Consolidation Durvalumab, for Subjects With Stage III Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Clin Lung Cancer). Design of a randomised phase II trial of hypofractionated (2.5 Gy) vs standard IMRT with chemo and durvalumab in stage III NSCLC; protocol, no results. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41687334/
- Naloxegol, an Oral Peripherally Acting Opioid Receptor Antagonist, Administered Concurrently with First-Line Systemic Therapy for Advanced Lung Adenocarcinoma (Alliance A221504): A Feasibility and Safety Study. (Cancers (Basel)). Alliance A221504 double-blind placebo-controlled RCT of naloxegol with first-line therapy terminated early (n=50): feasibility endpoint not met, signals of better emotional well-being and bowel function; small, underpowered. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41681846/
- Polish consensus on smoking cessation intervention within lung cancer screening 1. (Arch Med Sci). Polish expert consensus on smoking cessation within lung screening; national consensus statement without outcome data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41676427/
- Wedge resection versus segmentectomy for older patients with stage IA non-small cell lung cancer. (Indian J Thorac Cardiovasc Surg). Small open-label randomised study (n=50) in patients >=75 with stage IA NSCLC: wedge vs segmentectomy gave similar 2-year OS, more nodal upstaging with segmentectomy; underpowered. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41676053/
- Combination therapy with lorlatinib and mitogen-activated protein kinase pathway inhibition in previously treated ALK- or ROS1-rearranged lung cancer. (Lung Cancer). Phase IB trial (n=19) of lorlatinib plus binimetinib or TNO155 after ALK/ROS1 TKI resistance: ORR 6.7%, mPFS 51 days, terminated for slow accrual; small negative study of a resistance strategy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41671625/
- Effects of exercise on people living with advanced lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Support Care Cancer). Meta-analysis of 9 RCTs showing exercise improves VO2peak (SMD 0.38), strength, fatigue and QoL in advanced lung cancer; small trials, no prognosis data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41663540/
- Genomic biomarkers of immunotherapy plus chemotherapy in patients with advanced NSCLC: Insights from the phase 3 ORIENT-11 study. (iScience). Nine-gene ICPscore derived from the phase 3 ORIENT-11 trial predicting chemo-immunotherapy benefit, validated in OAK; retrospective biomarker analysis. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41660271/
- Impact of pleural lavage fluid volume on perioperative outcomes in non-small cell lung cancer patients undergoing video-assisted thoracoscopic lobectomy: a randomized controlled trial. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Single-centre RCT (n=406) of 250 vs 1000 mL pleural lavage at VATS lobectomy: similar postoperative fever (17% vs 23%, NS) and complications; procedural. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41659268/
- Pathologic Responses to Stereotactic Ablative Radiotherapy in Combination With Nivolumab for Early Stage NSCLC: A Phase 2 Study. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Single-arm phase II (n=25) of neoadjuvant SABR plus nivolumab in early NSCLC: pCR 79.2% (19/24) and MPR 83.3%; small single-arm study. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41657364/
- Comparison of the efficacy based on clinicopathological characteristics and the safety of first-line treatments for patients with advanced ALK rearrangement non-small cell lung cancer: a network meta-analysis. (Front Oncol). Network meta-analysis of RCTs of first-line ALK TKIs by clinicopathological subgroup; synthesis of existing trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41635404/
- SNF-CLIMEDIN: A Randomized Trial of Digital Support and Intervention in Patients With Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. A Hellenic Cooperative Oncology Group Study. (JCO Clin Cancer Inform). Hellenic Cooperative Oncology Group RCT (n=200) of an online digital AE-support platform in advanced NSCLC: improved rash and stomatitis and lower hospitalisation costs, no QoL difference; open-label. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41632937/
- Effect of patient education in improving quality of life, fatigue and anxiety in people diagnosed with lung cancer: systematic review. (Support Care Cancer). Meta-analysis (17 studies, 1,799 participants) suggesting patient education improves QoL, anxiety and fatigue in lung cancer, but certainty of evidence very low and heterogeneity high. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41632307/
- ctDNA guided immunotherapy in patients with advanced non-small cell lung cancer: a nationwide Danish, randomised, intervention study (PRELUCA-PRediction in LUng CAncer Treatment) - study protocol. (BMJ Open). Protocol of the nationwide Danish randomised PRELUCA trial of ctDNA-guided vs CT-guided immunotherapy evaluation in advanced NSCLC (OS primary endpoint); protocol of a major trial, no results. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41628935/
- Effect of preoperative inspiratory muscle training combined with education course on postoperative pulmonary complications in high-risk patients with lung cancer after video-assisted thoracoscopic surgery: a randomized controlled trial. (Contemp Clin Trials Commun). Small RCT of preoperative home inspiratory muscle training plus education before VATS stopped for futility at interim (n=56): no difference in postoperative pulmonary complications. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41626065/
- Patient-reported outcomes with a personalized follow-up program after lung cancer resection: A single-center randomized controlled trial. (Asia Pac J Oncol Nurs). Single-centre RCT (n=240) of a personalised PRO-based follow-up programme after lung resection improved QoL at 4 weeks and self-management efficacy; open-label, short follow-up. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41625083/
- Hepatotoxicity of new-generation ALK inhibitors versus crizotinib in patients with non-small cell lung cancer: A systematic review and meta-analysis. (iScience). Meta-analysis of 8 RCTs (2,114 patients) showing lower ALT elevation risk with new-generation ALK TKIs vs crizotinib; safety synthesis. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41623453/
- Effects of pulmonary rehabilitation in people with lung cancer after lung resection: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (J Cancer Surviv). Meta-analysis of 18 RCTs (n=1,795) showing post-resection pulmonary rehabilitation improves FVC, 6MWD and physical QoL; only 3 trials at low risk of bias. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41615632/
- Tislelizumab plus platinum and etoposide versus placebo plus platinum and etoposide as first-line treatment for extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer: patient-reported outcomes in the RATIONALE-312 trial. (Curr Med Res Opin). Prespecified PRO analysis of phase 3 RATIONALE-312 (n=457) showing improved GHS/QoL with tislelizumab + chemo in ES-SCLC; secondary endpoint, open interpretation, no TTD difference. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41614649/
- Encorafenib plus binimetinib versus dabrafenib plus trametinib for the first-line treatment of patients with BRAFV600E-mutant metastatic non-small cell lung cancer: a matching-adjusted indirect treatment comparison. (ESMO Open). Matching-adjusted indirect comparison of encorafenib/binimetinib (PHAROS) vs dabrafenib/trametinib in BRAF V600E NSCLC: PFS HR 0.47; unanchored indirect comparison of single-arm trials, industry-linked. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41604820/
- Immunomodulatory Effects of Multivitamin Complexes Containing Agaricus blazei in Patients Undergoing Curative Resection for Non-Small-Cell Lung Cancer: A Randomized, Double-Blind, Placebo-Controlled Multicenter Pilot Trial. (Biomedicines). Multicentre double-blind placebo-controlled pilot RCT (n=66) of a multivitamin/Agaricus supplement after NSCLC resection: NK activity recovery not significantly different; immune-subset findings exploratory. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41595590/
- Frailty, comorbidity and survival comparisons between populations eligible for screening according to risk factor versus risk score criteria: results from the Yorkshire Lung Screening Trial. (Thorax). Yorkshire Lung Screening Trial analysis (n=11,994 assessed) showing risk-model eligibility selects more frail and comorbid people than USPSTF2021 criteria; secondary analysis informing screening eligibility. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41593003/
- Efficacy and safety of anti-LAG-3 IBI110 in combination with sintilimab and chemotherapy for advanced squamous non-small cell lung cancer: a randomized phase II study. (Cancer Immunol Immunother). Randomised phase II (n=153) of anti-LAG-3 IBI110 + sintilimab + chemo in squamous NSCLC: no ORR/PFS/OS difference overall, signal only in LAG-3>=2% subgroup; negative primary result. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41591537/
- Adjuvant Chemotherapy with Traditional Chinese Herbal Granules Versus Placebo in Resected Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: Updated Survival Analysis of a Multicenter, Randomized, Double-Blind, Placebo-Controlled Trial. (Integr Cancer Ther). Updated survival of a multicentre double-blind placebo-controlled RCT (n=334) of TCM granules with adjuvant chemo: no significant DFS (HR 0.86, p=0.31) or OS difference; subgroup signals only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41589575/
- Patient-Reported Symptoms and Quality of Life With Sacituzumab Govitecan Versus Docetaxel in Metastatic NSCLC: The Phase 3, Randomized EVOKE-01 Trial. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Exploratory HRQoL analysis of phase 3 EVOKE-01 (n=603): sacituzumab govitecan delayed deterioration in dyspnoea and fatigue vs docetaxel; secondary endpoints of a trial whose OS was not significant. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41584718/
- Expert consensus on clinical practice for detecting fusion genes in non-small cell lung cancer using RNA-based next-generation sequencing. (Chin Med J (Engl)). Chinese expert consensus on RNA-based NGS fusion gene detection in NSCLC; abstract missing, judged from title as a diagnostic consensus. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41582867/
- Association between ALK tyrosine kinase inhibitor and the risk of interstitial lung disease and pneumonitis in non-small cell lung cancer patients: a systematic review. (BMC Cancer). Network meta-analysis of RCTs on ILD/pneumonitis risk across ALK TKIs; safety synthesis. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41578213/
- Performance of smoking duration-based lung cancer screening eligibility criteria: a comparative modeling study. (J Natl Cancer Inst). CISNET comparative modelling (JNCI) showing a 35-year smoking-duration screening criterion performs similarly to current USPSTF criteria; model-based. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41578016/
- Economic Evaluation of Tobacco Treatments From the Screen ASSIST Lung Cancer Screening Trial. (JAMA Netw Open). Prospective economic evaluation of the factorial Screen ASSIST RCT (n=642) of smoking-cessation strategies within lung screening; cost data, cessation in people without cancer. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41575745/
- Recommendations on the management of kinase inhibitor-associated hyperlipidemia in patients with lung cancer. (Cancer Treat Rev). Multidisciplinary recommendations for managing kinase-inhibitor (notably lorlatinib) hyperlipidaemia in lung cancer; practical consensus-style guidance, not new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41564805/
- Effect of Lung Cancer Screening, Smoking Cessation, and Cessation Smartphone App to Health-Related Quality of Life Among Heavy Smokers: Randomized Controlled Trial. (J Med Internet Res). Exploratory HRQoL analysis of a Finnish RCT (n=200) of app-based vs standard smoking cessation within LDCT screening; people without cancer, exploratory endpoint. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41557895/
- Evidence-based indications for proton therapy in adults determined using the GRADE approach. (Clin Transl Oncol). Spanish HTA using GRADE to define evidence-based indications for proton therapy in adults; lung only one of several indications. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41557143/
- Bronchial Arterial Chemoembolization Combined with Tislelizumab for Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: An Exploratory, Prospective, Single-Arm, Phase II Trial. (J Vasc Interv Radiol). Single-arm phase II (n=30) of bronchial arterial chemoembolisation plus tislelizumab in stage III NSCLC ineligible for standard therapy: mPFS 10.5 months, ORR 60%; small, single-arm. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41548595/
- An FDA analysis of the association of tumor growth rate, overall survival, and progression-free survival in patients with metastatic non-small cell lung cancer. (Oncologist). FDA pooled analysis of 10,495 patients from 22 RCTs linking tumour growth rate (g) with OS and PFS in metastatic NSCLC; retrospective endpoint research. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41546388/
- Perioperative penpulimab-based combination therapy in patients with resectable non-small cell lung cancer (ALTER-L043): an open-label, multicenter, randomized, phase II trial. (Signal Transduct Target Ther). Randomised phase II ALTER-L043 (n=90) of perioperative penpulimab + anlotinib +/- chemo: MPR 76% and pCR 52% with the triplet; small arms, no survival data, not powered for comparison. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41545337/
- Primary and secondary pseudo-stability and progression after atezolizumab with and without bevacizumab. (J Immunother Cancer). Post hoc analysis of 2,980 patients across 8 atezolizumab RCTs characterising pseudo-stability/progression (4-12%); multi-tumour, exploratory. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41545308/
- KRAS-mutant advanced NSCLC: efficacy and clinical outcomes from network meta-analysis and real-world evidence. (Lung Cancer). Bayesian network meta-analysis of 13 RCTs plus real-world data for KRAS-mutant NSCLC treatment choices; indirect comparisons. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41544598/
- Preclinical characterization and first-in-human, phase I trial of the novel ALK inhibitor dirozalkib in advanced non-small cell lung cancer. (Eur J Cancer). Preclinical work and first-in-human phase I (n=114) of new ALK inhibitor dirozalkib: ORR ~46% overall and 89.3% in TKI-naive patients at 500 mg; early-phase, single-arm. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41539180/
- Olaparib in Patients With Solid Tumors With ATM Alterations: Results From the Targeted Agent and Profiling Utilization Registry (TAPUR) Study. (JCO Precis Oncol). TAPUR phase II basket cohorts of olaparib in ATM-altered tumours: lung cohort (n=20) met its disease-control signal (45%); very small cohort, non-randomised. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41538762/
- Feasibility and Preliminary Efficacy of Aerobic Acute Exercise Prior to Immunotherapy and Chemotherapy Infusion in Patients with Metastatic Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Randomized Controlled Trial. (J Clin Med). Feasibility RCT ERICA (n=26) of acute aerobic exercise before each immunochemotherapy infusion in metastatic NSCLC: 81% adherence, no exercise-related AEs; tiny pilot, hypothesis-generating. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41517582/
- Population-Based Outcomes of Single-Fraction Stereotactic Ablative Radiotherapy for Early Stage Non-small Cell Lung Cancer. (Clin Oncol (R Coll Radiol)). Population-based multi-institution study (n=166) of single-fraction SABR for early NSCLC: 2-year local failure 7.1%, OS 81.5%, low toxicity; retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41512571/
- Malignancy Detection Rates of [68Ga]Ga-SSO120 PET/CT in Patients with Small Cell Lung Cancer and Large Cell Neuroendocrine Lung Cancer. (J Nucl Med). Prospective phase 2 imaging trial of [68Ga]Ga-SSO120 PET/CT in 19 SCLC/LCNEC patients; lesion-detection was 95% at patient level but fewer lesions than CT; very small, diagnostic only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41506802/
- The inverse palliative care law in advanced lung disease: a mixed-methods systematic review and meta-analysis. (EClinicalMedicine). Mixed-methods systematic review/meta-analysis showing socioeconomic inequalities in palliative-care access in advanced lung disease including lung cancer; observational evidence. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41502923/
- Clinical impact of TP53 classifications in previously treated advanced driver-negative non-small cell lung cancer: A biomarker analysis of the OAK and POPLAR randomized clinical trials. (Lung Cancer). Biomarker analysis of the OAK and POPLAR RCTs exploring refined plasma TP53 mutation classes as prognostic/predictive for atezolizumab vs docetaxel; exploratory post hoc. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41500083/
- Phase II trial of neoadjuvant camrelizumab and apatinib in resectable NSCLC: 3-year survival outcomes and dynamic circulating tumor DNA analyses. (Eur J Cancer). Single-arm phase 2 of neoadjuvant camrelizumab plus apatinib in resectable stage IIA-IIIB NSCLC: 3-year EFS 58.9%, OS 92.3%; ctDNA clearance associated with EFS; uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41500061/
- Interrelationship Between Patient and Family Caregiver Outcomes Following Lung Cancer Surgery: A Secondary Analysis of a Randomized Controlled Trial. (Psychooncology). Secondary analysis of an RCT of 190 patient-caregiver dyads after lung cancer surgery on interrelated outcomes; secondary, not the primary intervention effect. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41499244/
- Efficacy and safety of oral vinorelbine with concurrent radiotherapy in unresectable stage III non-small cell lung cancer following neoadjuvant chemoimmunotherapy: a single-arm, phase 2 trial. (J Natl Cancer Cent). Single-arm phase 2 of oral vinorelbine with concurrent radiotherapy after neoadjuvant chemoimmunotherapy in unresectable stage III NSCLC; uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41497257/
- Real-world clinical utility of comprehensive genomic profiling in advanced solid tumors. (Nat Med). Nationwide Japanese real-world analysis of comprehensive genomic profiling in 54,185 patients with solid tumours, showing prognostic value of actionable alterations; pan-tumour, lung is a subset. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41495408/
- Real-world outcomes versus clinical trial results of first-line immunotherapy with or without chemotherapy in patients with metastatic non-small-cell lung cancer: the CORRELATE study. (ESMO Open). CORRELATE: Flatiron real-world comparison showing shorter OS/PFS than in RCTs for first-line immunotherapy in metastatic NSCLC; large retrospective cohort. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41494223/
- First-line immunotherapy-based regimens for metastatic non-small cell lung cancer: A network meta-analysis of landmark trials. (Tuberk Toraks). Network meta-analysis of five phase 3 first-line immunotherapy trials in metastatic NSCLC; RCT-based but limited network and regional journal. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41486797/
- Multicenter Real-World Data on First-Line Chemoimmunotherapy in Patients With Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer and Performance Status 2: WJOG18424L. (Clin Lung Cancer). WJOG18424L: multicentre real-world cohort of 2,425 patients assessing first-line chemoimmunotherapy in PS2 NSCLC with propensity matching; retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41483952/
- Efficiency and safety of sintilimab-based induction therapy in potentially resectable stage III non-small cell lung cancer (LungMate-009): an open-label, phase 2 trial. (EClinicalMedicine). LungMate-009 open-label phase 2 (n=97) of PD-L1-guided sintilimab-based induction in potentially resectable stage III NSCLC: ORR 82%, 60% resected, MPR 66% of resected, 24-month OS 88%; single-arm. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41477564/
- Longitudinal Analysis of Peripheral Blood CD4+ T-Cell Profiles and Clinical Outcomes in Metastatic Non-Small-Cell Lung Cancer Patients Following Bronchoscopic Cryotherapy and Pembrolizumab-Based Therapy. (Int J Mol Sci). Single-centre randomised study (n=76 analysed) adding bronchoscopic cryotherapy before first-line pembrolizumab: ORR 41.2% vs 16.7%, PFS/OS not significantly different; small, immune-profiling focus. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41977120/
- [Huangqi Guizhi Wuwu Decoction in the Treatment of Pemetrexed Intrathecal Chemotherapy-induced Neurotoxicity in NSCLC Patients with Leptomeningeal Metastases: A Randomized Controlled Trial]. (Zhongguo Fei Ai Za Zhi). Double-blind RCT (n=220) in NSCLC leptomeningeal metastases: a herbal decoction reduced intrathecal pemetrexed neurotoxicity (29.1% vs 51.8%) with longer PFS/OS reported; single-country TCM trial needing confirmation. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41975647/
- First-Line Sacituzumab Govitecan Plus Pembrolizumab and Carboplatin in Metastatic Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: Nonsquamous and Squamous Cohorts of the EVOKE-02 Study. (Clin Cancer Res). EVOKE-02 phase 2 cohorts (n=95) of first-line sacituzumab govitecan plus pembrolizumab and carboplatin: ORR 45.1% nonsquamous, 39.0% squamous, median PFS ~8 months, grade>=3 TEAEs 86%; single-arm. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41961582/
- Impact of tumor location on the efficacy of concurrent chemoradiotherapy for locally advanced non-small cell lung cancer. (Br J Cancer). Pooled individual-patient analysis of 1,165 patients from randomised phase 2/3 cCRT trials showing tumour location associated with PFS in locally advanced NSCLC; post hoc. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41957135/
- The Promise of Chemotherapy-Free Strategies in Advanced Driver-Negative NSCLC: A Systematic Review and Network Meta-Analysis of Antiangiogenic Combination Therapies. (Cancer Med). Network meta-analysis of RCTs of antiangiogenic combinations in driver-negative advanced NSCLC; RCT-based but indirect comparisons. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41952287/
- SUNRAY-01 trial protocol: an innovative study design of olomorasib and pembrolizumab with or without chemotherapy in KRAS G12C NSCLC. (Future Oncol). Design paper for SUNRAY-01, a major trial of olomorasib plus pembrolizumab with or without chemotherapy in KRAS G12C NSCLC; protocol only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41940628/
- Efficacy and safety of albumin-bound paclitaxel combined with simvastatin in the second-line treatment of small cell lung cancer: a phase II randomized controlled trial. (BMC Med). Small randomised phase 2 in second-line SCLC: nab-paclitaxel plus simvastatin improved DCR (92.9% vs 44.4%), ORR and PFS but not OS. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41937137/
- Comparing Nodal Upstaging Rates and Morbidity and Mortality of Common Nodal Sampling Guidelines in the STS General Thoracic Surgery Database. (Ann Thorac Surg). Large STS database analysis comparing nodal sampling standards for nodal upstaging and morbidity in resected NSCLC; registry-based. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41933779/
- New conversations in an old argument: Lessons learnt from the PIONEER randomised feasibility study in resectable stage III-N2 NSCLC. (Lung Cancer). PIONEER UK feasibility RCT (n=28) of surgical vs non-surgical treatment in resectable III-N2 NSCLC concluded a phase 3 is unlikely to be feasible; feasibility only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41930542/
- Significant Incidental Findings in the National Lung Screening Trial and Diagnosis of Extrapulmonary Cancer. (JAMA Netw Open). Secondary retrospective analysis of NLST participants on incidental findings and extrapulmonary cancer diagnoses; screening-trial derived but secondary. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41915394/
- Induction Serplulimab and Chemotherapy Followed by Chemoradiotherapy for Bulky Unresectable Stage III NSCLC: A Phase II Study (SUCCEED-01). (Cancer Immunol Res). SUCCEED-01 single-arm phase 2 (n=49) of induction serplulimab-chemotherapy before cCRT in bulky stage III NSCLC: ORR 77%, V20 reduced 11.4%, 12-month PFS 76%; small, uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41911546/
- Optimizing treatment and sequencing strategies for maximal survival and net health benefits in ALK-positive NSCLC: a systematic review and reconstructed individual patient data meta-analysis. (BMC Cancer). Reconstructed IPD meta-analysis of 27 ALK-positive phase 2/3 trials modelling sequencing; supports lorlatinib first-line but relies on cross-trial modelling. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41906076/
- Association Between Patient-Reported Outcomes and Overall Survival in Patients with Advanced NSCLC Treated with First-Line Cemiplimab-Based Therapy. (Cancers (Basel)). Pooled analysis of EMPOWER-Lung 1 and 3 phase 3 PRO data linking post-baseline PRO changes with OS on cemiplimab; post hoc. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41899521/
- Effects of a rehabilitation program based on home intelligent management platform guided among patient after lung cancer surgery: a randomized controlled trail. (Support Care Cancer). Small single-centre RCT (n=50) of a home intelligent-management rehabilitation platform after lung cancer surgery on respiratory function, psychology and QoL; underpowered. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41896472/
- First-Line Cemiplimab for Locally Advanced NSCLC: Updated Subgroup Analyses From the EMPOWER-Lung 1 and EMPOWER-Lung 3 Trials. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Updated subgroup analyses of locally advanced NSCLC patients in phase 3 EMPOWER-Lung 1 and 3 receiving first-line cemiplimab; post hoc subgroup. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41869512/
- Multidisciplinary international expert consensus recommendations on tissue acquisition in non-small cell lung cancer. (EBioMedicine). International multidisciplinary expert consensus on tissue acquisition for NSCLC biomarker testing; procedural recommendations, industry funded. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41861516/
- Phase III meta-analysis of first-line chemo-immunotherapy in ES-SCLC: Survival benefit without detectable class-level differences. (Lung Cancer). Updated meta-analysis of phase 3 RCTs of first-line chemo-immunotherapy in ES-SCLC showing consistent survival benefit without PD-1 vs PD-L1 class differences. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41855713/
- Non-Pharmacological Interventions for Cough in Patients With Lung Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (J Clin Nurs). Meta-analysis of RCTs of non-pharmacological interventions for cough in lung cancer; synthesis of small trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41834268/
- Tumor Purity as a Prognostic and Predictive Biomarker of Postoperative Radiation Therapy Outcomes in Stage IIIA-N2 Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Transcriptomic Analysis From the Lung ART Trial. (Int J Radiat Oncol Biol Phys). Transcriptomic analysis (n=285) from the Lung ART RCT exploring tumour purity as prognostic/predictive for PORT benefit in IIIA-N2 NSCLC; exploratory. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41833910/
- Long-Term Analysis of One Versus Three Fractions of Stereotactic Body Radiation Therapy for Peripheral Stage I to II Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Randomized, Multi-Institution, Phase 2 Trial. (Int J Radiat Oncol Biol Phys). Randomised phase 2 (n=98) long-term follow-up: 30 Gy/1 fraction SBRT was similar to 60 Gy/3 fractions for toxicity, local control, PFS and OS in peripheral early NSCLC; small, not powered for equivalence. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41833909/
- Durvalumab combined with concurrent chemoradiotherapy in patients with limited-stage small cell lung cancer: A prospective, single-arm, phase 2 clinical trial. (Cancer). Single-arm phase 2 (n=51) of durvalumab concurrent with chemoradiotherapy in LS-SCLC: median PFS 17.0 months, 3-year OS 49.6%; uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41832629/
- Prophylactic Recombinant Human Thrombopoietin Prevents Cancer Therapy-Induced Thrombocytopenia During Concurrent Chemoradiation Therapy in Limited-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Prospective, Multicenter, Phase II Clinical Trial. (Int J Radiat Oncol Biol Phys). Single-arm multicentre phase 2 of prophylactic rhTPO to prevent thrombocytopenia during cCRT in LS-SCLC; supportive care, uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41831791/
- Domvanalimab combined with zimberelimab as first-line treatment in patients with PD-L1-high, advanced non-small cell lung cancer: Results from the randomized phase 2 ARC-10 study, Part1. (Lung Cancer). Randomised phase 2 ARC-10 Part 1 (n=98) in PD-L1-high NSCLC: domvanalimab plus zimberelimab gave median PFS 11.5 vs 6.2 months for zimberelimab (HR 0.69, CI crossing 1); small, not definitive. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41830668/
- Pan-tumor activity of olomorasib, a next-generation KRAS G12C inhibitor in KRAS G12C-mutant advanced solid tumors: a first-in-human study. (Nat Commun). First-in-human phase 1 (n=195) of olomorasib in KRAS G12C solid tumours with RP2D 150 mg BID; early-phase, pan-tumour. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41820335/
- First-line therapies with maintenance regimens for driver gene-negative advanced non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC): a systematic review and network meta-analysis of 61 randomized trials. (J Thorac Dis). Bayesian NMA of 61 RCTs of first-line regimens with maintenance in driver-negative advanced NSCLC; RCT-based indirect comparisons. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41816410/
- Optimal duration of adjuvant immunotherapy after neoadjuvant chemoimmunotherapy in resectable non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (J Thorac Dis). Meta-analysis of perioperative phase 2/3 RCTs comparing 12/13- vs 6/8-cycle adjuvant PD-1/PD-L1 after neoadjuvant chemoimmunotherapy; indirect cross-trial comparison. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41816378/
- Pulmonary complications with individualised vs. fixed positive end-expiratory pressure in older patients recovering from lung cancer surgery: a randomised trial. (Anaesthesia). RCT (n=400) in older adults having lung cancer surgery: EIT-guided individualised PEEP improved intraoperative mechanics but did not reduce postoperative pulmonary complications (28% vs 25%); anaesthetic technique. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41815001/
- The Effect of Early Neuromuscular Electrical Stimulation and Early Mobilization on Preventing the Intensive Care Unit-Acquired Weakness in Lung Cancer Resection Patients: A Randomized Controlled Trial. (Technol Cancer Res Treat). Single-centre three-arm RCT in lung resection patients: NMES and early mobilisation each reduced ICU-acquired weakness vs control, without shortening stay; sample size not given in abstract. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42139552/
- Impact of Anti-Inflammatory Drug Therapy on the Efficacy of First-Line Atezolizumab Treatment in Patients With Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: An Integrated Analysis of Five Randomized Controlled Trials. (Thorac Cancer). Patient-level pooled analysis of five IMpower RCTs (n=3,781) on acetaminophen/NSAID exposure and atezolizumab efficacy; post hoc observational exposure. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42138243/
- Atezolizumab + Chemotherapy in Older Patients With Lung Cancer in Japan. (Cancer Sci). J-TAIL-2 prospective observational study (n=1,217) of atezolizumab-chemotherapy in Japanese NSCLC/ES-SCLC including trial-ineligible and older patients; non-randomised. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42135591/
- Morning versus afternoon administration of immune checkpoint inhibitors in metastatic non-small-cell lung cancer. (J Immunother Cancer). Target-trial emulation in VA records (n=1,965 analysed) finding PM vs AM ICI infusion associated with worse OS (HR 1.15) in stage IV NSCLC, with no effect in chemotherapy controls; observational. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42134900/
- Comparative effectiveness of first-line targeted therapies in ALK-positive non-small cell lung cancer: real-world evidence of tyrosine kinase inhibitors. (Lung Cancer). US claims-based comparative effectiveness of first-line ALK TKIs with propensity scores; large real-world but retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42134157/
- ENTRÉE Lung Platform Trial Sub-study 1: Phase II Randomized Efficacy and Safety Analysis of Feladilimab Plus Docetaxel Versus Docetaxel Monotherapy in Advanced/Recurrent NSCLC. (Clin Lung Cancer). ENTREE Lung randomised phase 2 sub-study of feladilimab plus docetaxel vs docetaxel after IO and platinum; randomised but small phase 2. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42120266/
- Simplified perioperative serplulimab and chemotherapy for resectable squamous NSCLC: a phase II trial with biomarker analysis. (J Immunother Cancer). Single-arm exploratory phase 2 of simplified four-cycle perioperative serplulimab-chemotherapy in resectable squamous NSCLC with biomarkers; small, uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42114951/
- Staging Modalities in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: Evidence-Based Guideline and Systematic Review by the Brazilian Group of Thoracic Oncology. (Clin Lung Cancer). Brazilian GRADE-based guideline and systematic review on NSCLC staging modalities; national guideline on diagnostics. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42114171/
- A Multi-Institutional Randomized Phase III Trial of Neoadjuvant Chemoimmunotherapy Followed By Surgery Versus Upfront Surgery in Patients With Resectable Clinical Stage II-III Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: JCOG2317 (NATCH-ICI). (Clin Lung Cancer). Protocol of JCOG2317 (NATCH-ICI), a phase 3 RCT comparing neoadjuvant chemoimmunotherapy with upfront surgery in stage II-III NSCLC; no results. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42107198/
- A Noddings' caring theory-based intervention to enhance coping with death competence in advanced lung cancer patients: a randomized controlled trial. (Support Care Cancer). Small RCT (n=70) of a caring-theory intervention improving coping with death and distress in advanced lung cancer inpatients; short follow-up. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42101688/
- Sequential chemo-durvalumab, reduced-dose RT, and consolidation durvalumab for unresectable stage III NSCLC unfit for PACIFIC regimen (DEDALUS trial). (JNCI Cancer Spectr). DEDALUS phase 2 of sequential chemo-durvalumab, hypofractionated RT and durvalumab consolidation in stage III NSCLC unfit for PACIFIC; single-arm, safety endpoint. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42093085/
- Low-dose radiotherapy synergizes with PD-1 blockade to achieve durable survival in advanced NSCLC through antitumor neutrophil programming. (Signal Transduct Target Ther). Phase 1 TRIDENT trial (n=29) of low-dose plus high-dose RT with PD-1 blockade in PD-L1-positive advanced NSCLC reporting median OS 51.3 months; very small, uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42091852/
- Mature Outcomes of 61.2 Gy Concomitant Boost Thoracic Radiation Therapy in Limited-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer: CALGB 30610 (Alliance)/RTOG 0538. (Int J Radiat Oncol Biol Phys). Mature outcomes of the discontinued 61.2 Gy concomitant-boost arm of randomised CALGB 30610/RTOG 0538 in LS-SCLC; single-arm analysis of a dropped arm. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42086125/
- Nicotine replacement therapy starter packs and gain-framed messages to promote smoking cessation in lung cancer screening: a randomized trial. (Am J Respir Crit Care Med). Randomised trial of NRT starter packs and gain-framed messages for smoking cessation within lung screening (AJRCCM); abstract missing, judged from title. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42085282/
- Time-of-Day Immunotherapy Administration and Outcomes in Advanced Cancers: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (JAMA Netw Open). JAMA Netw Open meta-analysis of time-of-day ICI administration and outcomes across advanced cancers; largely observational studies. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42084869/
- Induction chemotherapy plus bevacizumab or radiotherapy in N2 non-squamous non-small cell lung cancer: 5-year results of a randomized phase 2 trial. (JTCVS Open). Randomized phase 2 selection trial (PIT-1, n=82) in N2 non-squamous NSCLC; 5-year OS 63.5% vs 57.2% for induction chemo+bevacizumab vs chemoradiotherapy, no significant difference; small and older regimen. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42079970/
- Preoperative circulating tumor DNA detection and occult lymph node metastases in clinical stage I lung cancer: The lung DETECT study. (JTCVS Open). Prospective multicentre cohort (Lung DETECT, n=153) linking preoperative tumour-informed ctDNA to occult nodal metastases in clinical stage I NSCLC; small number of events. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42079928/
- KRASG12C inhibitors versus chemotherapy alone for KRASG12C-mutated non-small cell lung cancer: a pooled analysis of CodeBreaK 200 and KRYSTAL-12 trials. (Front Oncol). Pooled analysis of two phase 3 RCTs (CodeBreaK 200, KRYSTAL-12; n=798): KRAS G12C inhibitors improved PFS vs chemotherapy (HR 0.62) and CNS-PFS (HR 0.55); adds little beyond the trials themselves. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42063697/
- Bridging the lung cancer screening eligibility gap: evaluation of guideline applicability in asymptomatic patients. (Mil Med Res). Large multicentre hospital-based study (n=106,266 resected asymptomatic lung cancers in China) showing only 8.8% met USPSTF 2021 screening criteria; retrospective, surgical population only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42063463/
- Early ctDNA Stratifies Survival in Locally Advanced and Oligometastatic Lung Cancer Treated with Radiotherapy. (Clin Cancer Res). Prospective phase 2 trial (n=26) with external validation (n=94, n=309) showing baseline and mid-treatment ctDNA tumour fraction stratified OS/PFS after radiotherapy; small trial cohort, prognostic only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42059900/
- Efficacy and Safety of Acupuncture for Pain in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Meta-Analysis. (J Pain Res). Meta-analysis of 11 RCTs (n=745) suggesting acupuncture reduces pain in NSCLC; small trials of variable quality. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42051753/
- Pembrolizumab combined with chemotherapy for advanced or metastatic non-small cell lung cancer with low PD-L1 expression: a systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials. (Immunotherapy). Meta-analysis of 4 RCTs (n=2,413): pembrolizumab plus chemotherapy improved OS in PD-L1 <50% NSCLC (HR 0.67) with low certainty of evidence. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42046364/
- A Phase II Study of Stereotactic Ablative Radiotherapy plus Atezolizumab plus Tiragolumab in Treatment-Naïve Patients with Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer. (Clin Cancer Res). Single-arm single-centre phase 2 (SKYROCKET, n=41) of SBRT plus atezolizumab-tiragolumab in PD-L1+ NSCLC; median PFS 9.3 months with short follow-up and no control arm. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42008740/
- Surgical outcomes after neoadjuvant chemoimmunotherapy for resectable NSCLC: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Eur J Surg Oncol). Meta-analysis of 27 prospective trials (n=2,691) on surgical outcomes after neoadjuvant ICI-based therapy: postoperative complications 27%, mortality 1%; heterogeneous single-arm and randomized trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42000405/
- Sex-based differences in effectiveness of immune checkpoint inhibitors in the treatment of advanced non-small-cell lung cancer: systematic review and meta-analysis. (Thorax). Meta-analysis of 55 RCTs (n=28,550): ICIs improved OS similarly in men (HR 0.75) and women (HR 0.81), with a larger PFS benefit in men; trial-level subgroup data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41997852/
- Stereotactic Versus Conventional Radiotherapy in Medically Inoperable Stage I Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Bayesian Reanalysis of the LUSTRE Randomized Trial. (Clin Lung Cancer). Bayesian reanalysis of the LUSTRE randomized trial (n=233) suggesting a 91% probability that SBRT improves local control vs conventional RT in inoperable stage I NSCLC; post hoc reanalysis of an inconclusive trial. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41997828/
- Disease characteristics and treatment outcomes in patients with resected early-stage ALK-positive non-small cell lung cancer from the randomized ALINA trial. (Lung Cancer). Exploratory subgroup analysis of phase 3 ALINA showing adjuvant alectinib DFS benefit consistent across AJCC 8th-edition stage, nodal status and tumour size; no new primary endpoint data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41996761/
- Efficacy of first-line immune checkpoint inhibitors in advanced non-small-cell lung cancer with or without brain metastases: a systematic review and network meta-analysis. (Front Oncol). Bayesian network meta-analysis of 12 first-line ICI RCTs (n=7,122) stratified by brain metastases; ICI-chemo improved OS in patients with brain metastases (HR 0.57); indirect comparisons. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41994648/
- Efficacy of PD-1/PD-L1 inhibitors combined with multi-targeted anti-angiogenic TKIs in advanced or metastatic NSCLC: A meta-analysis based on RCTs. (Front Oncol). Meta-analysis of 6 RCTs (n=2,787): PD-1/PD-L1 plus anti-angiogenic TKIs improved PFS (HR 0.82) but not OS (HR 0.97) in advanced NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41994630/
- Equivalence of neoadjuvant and perioperative therapies in patients with stage III non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (J Thorac Dis). Reconstructed-IPD meta-analysis of 7 trials (n=1,436) finding no significant EFS/OS difference between neoadjuvant and perioperative immunotherapy in stage III NSCLC; indirect cross-trial comparison. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41988329/
- Rescue by radiotherapy and anti-CTLA4/PD-1 after failure of anti-PD-1 therapy in patients with metastatic NSCLC: the phase II RECLAIM trial. (J Immunother Cancer). Single-arm phase 2 (RECLAIM, n=31) of ipilimumab-nivolumab plus subablative radiotherapy after anti-PD-1 failure in PD-L1-low/negative NSCLC; low early ORR (7-10%), small uncontrolled study. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42331381/
- Prognostic associations between patient-reported outcomes and overall survival among patients with advanced squamous non-small cell lung cancer: results from the RATIONALE-307 trial. (Qual Life Res). Secondary PRO analysis of phase 3 RATIONALE-307 (n=328) linking patient-reported symptoms to OS with tislelizumab-chemo in squamous NSCLC; exploratory modelling. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42319531/
- Effect of histology on the efficacy of first-line immune checkpoint inhibitors in advanced non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and network meta-analysis. (Front Immunol). Network meta-analysis of 41 phase 3 RCTs (n=23,871) suggesting first-line ICI efficacy varies by histology; indirect comparisons. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42317330/
- Deep learning of CT imaging predicts PD-L1 expression and immunotherapy response in metastatic NSCLC: A multi-center study. (Cancer Lett). Retrospective multicentre development of a CT deep-learning model (n=972, external validation incl. LONESTAR phase 3 cohort) predicting PD-L1 >=50% with AUC 0.78-0.84; retrospective, not prospectively tested. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42314966/
- Short-term quality of life after metastases-directed SBRT: Results of the ESTRO & EORTC OligoCare cohort. (Radiother Oncol). Prospective OligoCare registry cohort (n=2,041; 450 with follow-up QoL) describing HRQoL after metastasis-directed SBRT across tumour types including NSCLC; high attrition. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42314835/
- Programmed-death 1/programmed-death ligand 1 inhibitors plus chemotherapy versus chemotherapy for first-line treatment of advanced driver-gene negative non-squamous non-small cell lung cancer: An updated systematic review and meta-analysis. (Indian J Cancer). Meta-analysis of 9 RCTs (n=3,443): first-line PD-1/PD-L1 plus chemotherapy improved PFS (HR 0.57) and OS (HR 0.69) in driver-negative non-squamous NSCLC; search only to 2023. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42301654/
- The esketamine-based multimodal low-opioid anaesthesia improves quality of recovery in elderly patients undergoing radical lung cancer surgery: a randomized controlled trial. (Front Pharmacol). Single-centre RCT (n=144) showing esketamine-based low-opioid anaesthesia improved recovery quality in elderly lung cancer surgery patients; short-term endpoints. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42292822/
- Long-term outcomes of ALK inhibitors in metastatic ALK-positive non-small cell lung cancer: an updated indirect comparison using reconstructed patient-level data. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Indirect comparison of 7 first-line ALK TKI RCTs with reconstructed IPD: lorlatinib showed superior PFS vs alectinib (HR 0.61) and other next-generation ALK TKIs; not head-to-head. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42291358/
- Cadonilimab plus chemotherapy as first-line treatment in PD-L1-negative advanced non-small cell lung cancer: a phase II clinical trial. (Nat Commun). Single-arm phase 2 (n~50) of cadonilimab (PD-1/CTLA-4 bispecific) plus chemotherapy in PD-L1-negative NSCLC: 12-month PFS 42.1% met threshold, ORR 66%, but grade>=3 TRAEs 52%; uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42285994/
- Female underrepresentation and adverse events reporting biases in lung cancer clinical trials: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (J Natl Cancer Cent). Meta-research of 636 lung cancer drug RCTs (n=265,989) quantifying female underrepresentation and AE reporting differences; trial-level analysis. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42282126/
- Study of the blood-brain barrier-penetrating KRAS G12C inhibitor JMKX1899 in KRAS G12C-mutated NSCLC with brain metastases. (J Natl Cancer Cent). Preclinical study of brain-penetrant KRAS G12C inhibitor JMKX1899 with brain-metastasis xenograft efficacy and early clinical observations; clinical data limited. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42282123/
- Real-World Emulation of Landmark Lung Cancer Trials: A Registry-Based Reconstruction of KN189, KN407, IMpower133, and PACIFIC. (Cancers (Basel)). Registry-based target trial emulation (n=4,015) reproducing KEYNOTE-189/407, IMpower133 and PACIFIC benefits in routine practice (e.g. eKN189 OS HR 0.59); observational. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42279336/
- The antibiotic-immunotherapy interaction in NSCLC: a systematic review and meta-analysis of 54,250 patients across three outcomes. (Cancer Treat Rev). Meta-analysis of 41 studies (n=54,250): antibiotic exposure linked to worse OS (HR 1.47) on ICIs in NSCLC, but RCT post hoc estimates non-significant, suggesting confounding. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42269396/
- Efficacy of alectinib for ALK-positive NSCLC according to tumor burden and body mass index: A pooled analysis of the randomized phase III trials ALEX and J-ALEX. (Eur J Cancer). Exploratory IPD pooled analysis of phase 3 ALEX and J-ALEX (n=500): high tumour burden predicted shorter PFS/OS and modified alectinib's PFS benefit vs crizotinib (interaction p=0.002); post hoc. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42269244/
- Stage II Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: Upfront Surgery or Neoadjuvant Chemoimmunotherapy. (Ann Thorac Surg). Reconstructed-IPD comparison of neoadjuvant chemoimmunotherapy trial patients (n=275) with upfront-surgery TNM database patients in stage II NSCLC; cross-source comparison prone to bias. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42264017/
- Multidisciplinary Approach to Sexual Dysfunction in Persons With Lung Cancer-Spanish Lung Cancer Group Expert Consensus Statement. (JTO Clin Res Rep). Spanish Lung Cancer Group Delphi expert consensus on managing sexual dysfunction in lung cancer; supportive-care recommendations largely expert-opinion based. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42254938/
- Final Analysis of Neoadjuvant Sintilimab Plus Chemotherapy in IB-IIIA Non-Small-Cell Lung Cancer: Phase 2 neoSCORE Trial. (Cancer Sci). Final analysis of randomized phase 2 neoSCORE (n=60): two vs three cycles of neoadjuvant sintilimab-chemo gave similar 4-year DFS (53.8% vs 55.2%) and OS; small trial not powered for survival. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42252705/
- Stage III Non-small Cell Lung Cancer Resectability in a Country With Limited Diagnostic and Therapeutic Resources: A Colombian Expert Consensus. (Cancer Control). Colombian multidisciplinary expert consensus (ESMO methodology) on stage III NSCLC resectability in resource-limited settings; regional, expert-opinion based. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42249675/
- Neoadjuvant Chemoimmunotherapy in Resectable NSCLC With PD-L1 Expression < 1%: A Systematic Review and Network Meta-Analysis of Randomized Controlled Trials. (Clin Lung Cancer). Bayesian network meta-analysis of 8 RCTs (1,012 PD-L1 <1% patients): no perioperative chemoimmunotherapy regimen significantly improved EFS or OS vs chemotherapy in PD-L1-negative resectable NSCLC; subgroup-derived data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42247797/
- Treatments for metastatic non-small cell lung cancer: a pathways pilot with systematic review, evidence synthesis and economic model. (Health Technol Assess). NICE-commissioned HTA pilot with systematic review, network meta-analysis and pathway economic model for metastatic NSCLC without targetable drivers; modelling-focused. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42246395/
- Effectiveness of carboplatin vs cisplatin in lung cancer chemo-immunotherapy: A systematic review and meta-analysis. (Tumori). Network meta-analysis of 11 chemo-ICI RCTs (n=6,125) finding no significant difference between carboplatin and cisplatin backbones; indirect, subgroup-derived. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42237664/
- Participant-Reported Preference for Pembrolizumab Administered Subcutaneously or Intravenously: A Randomized, Open-Label, Phase II Study. (JCO Oncol Pract). Randomized crossover phase 2 (n=147, mixed tumours incl. PD-L1>=50% NSCLC): 65% preferred subcutaneous over IV pembrolizumab; preference endpoint, short exposure. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42234938/
- Comparative efficacy of tarlatamab and second-line therapies in extensive-stage small cell lung cancer: A network meta-analysis. (Lung Cancer). Bayesian network meta-analysis suggesting tarlatamab improves OS vs historical second-line ES-SCLC regimens (HR 0.50-0.62); indirect comparison with historical trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42229339/
- Standard Versus Reduced Sotorasib Dosing in KRAS G12C-Mutated Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis-Have We Answered the Dosing Question? (JCO Oncol Pract). Meta-analysis of 10 mostly non-randomized studies comparing standard (960 mg) vs reduced sotorasib starting doses: ORR 33% vs 26%; few reduced-dose patients (n=130). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42224643/
- Efficacy and safety of subcutaneous and intravenous administration of atezolizumab in patients with non-small cell lung cancer, including early-stage, locally advanced or metastatic disease: Updated results of the IMscin001 and IMscin002 randomized studies. (Lung Cancer). Updated results of randomized IMscin001 (n=371) and IMscin002: subcutaneous and IV atezolizumab gave similar OS (10.9 vs 10.1 months) and safety in NSCLC; secondary/exploratory endpoints of a formulation study. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42214243/
- Pooled survival and cost-effectiveness analysis of immune checkpoint inhibitors-based for metastatic non-small-cell lung cancer with PD-L1 lower than 1. (Health Econ Rev). Pooled analysis of PD-L1 <1% subgroups from four RCTs (n=1,256) showing ICI-based therapy improved OS (HR 0.69) with cost-effectiveness modelling; reconstructed/exploratory data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42207389/
- Mature Outcomes and Patterns of Failure in the Phase II FLARE-RT Trial of Biological Image-Guided and Risk-Adaptive Chemoradiation for Unresectable NSCLC. (Clin Cancer Res). Mature outcomes of nonrandomized multicentre phase 2 FLARE-RT (n=49) testing PET-response-adapted dose-escalated chemoradiation in unresectable NSCLC; single-arm, small. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42207168/
- Nonpharmacological dyspnoea-relief strategies in lung cancer: a systematic review and network meta-analysis. (Eur Respir Rev). Network meta-analysis of 46 RCTs (n=4,138) of nonpharmacological dyspnoea strategies in lung cancer: acupressure, fan and multicomponent interventions ranked highest; heterogeneous small trials. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42203238/
- Sequential chemo-immunotherapy followed by standard versus reduced thoracic radiotherapy for older and/or frail stage III non-small-cell lung cancer: A randomized open-label cohort trial. (PLoS Med). Randomized non-comparative phase 2 (n=56, single centre) of sequential chemo-immunotherapy with standard vs reduced thoracic RT in older/frail stage III NSCLC: 1-year PFS 84.3% vs 70.7%; small, not powered for comparison. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42201929/
- Efficacy of PD-1/PD-L1 plus CTLA-4 inhibitors in advanced/metastatic NSCLC: a meta-analysis based on RCTs. (Front Immunol). Meta-analysis of 10 RCTs (n=6,369): PD-1/PD-L1 plus CTLA-4 inhibitors improved OS (HR 0.84) and PFS (HR 0.78) vs controls in advanced NSCLC. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42199400/
- Durvalumab plus anlotinib versus durvalumab alone as maintenance treatment in extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer (DURABLE): a multicenter, randomized, phase II trial and biomarker analysis. (Nat Commun). Randomized phase 2 DURABLE (n=66): durvalumab plus anlotinib maintenance improved PFS vs durvalumab alone in ES-SCLC (5.4 vs 1.9 months, HR 0.64, p=0.12 against a lenient 0.20 threshold); small, more grade 3-4 toxicity. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42185290/
- Predicting Radiation-Induced Esophagitis in Locally Advanced NSCLC Using Baseline Clinical Factors: A Model From the Japan Lung Cancer Society Integrated Phase II-III Database. (Clin Lung Cancer). Pooled analysis of 1,288 patients from 8 Japanese randomized chemoradiotherapy trials developing a predictive model for grade>=2 radiation esophagitis (c-statistic 0.85); internal model, retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42173719/
- Long-Term Outcomes After Proton Therapy Versus Intensity Modulated Photon Radiation Therapy With Concurrent Chemotherapy for Locally Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: Post Hoc Analysis of a Prospective Randomized Trial. (Int J Radiat Oncol Biol Phys). Long-term post hoc analysis of a randomized trial (n=149) of proton therapy vs IMRT with chemotherapy in locally advanced NSCLC: similar grade>=3 pneumonitis (10.5% vs 10.9%); modest follow-up. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42173326/
- [Expert consensus on adverse events management of highly selective RET-TKIs (2026 edition)]. (Zhonghua Zhong Liu Za Zhi). Chinese expert consensus on managing adverse events of selective RET TKIs (pralsetinib, selpercatinib); practical toxicity guidance rather than efficacy recommendations. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42161875/
- Pembrolizumab with or without chemotherapy among older adults with advanced lung adenocarcinoma: a national, nonrandomized open-label phase II trial (Alliance A171901). (J Natl Cancer Inst). Nonrandomized multicentre phase 2 (Alliance A171901, n=95, age>=70): solicited grade>=3 AEs in 25.6% with pembrolizumab alone vs 42.3% with chemo-pembrolizumab in older adults with lung adenocarcinoma; nonrandomized arms. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42161344/
- Incidence and lung cancer probability of new nodules in the UK lung screening trial. (Eur J Cancer). Secondary analysis of the UKLS screening RCT (875 with follow-up CT): new solid nodules at 12 months carried 7.4% participant-level malignancy risk, stratified by volume; few cancers (n=5). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42155364/
- Phase II Multicenter Clinical Trial of Intraoperative Molecular Imaging with Abenacianine During Lung Cancer Surgery. (Ann Surg Oncol). Multicentre phase 2 (n=89) of cathepsin-targeted NIR imaging agent abenacianine during lung cancer surgery: 45% had a clinically significant event, e.g. lesions not found by standard methods in 37%; single-arm. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42502096/
- [Expert consensus on iruplinalkib for the treatment of ALK-positive non-small cell lung cancer (2026 edition)]. (Zhonghua Zhong Liu Za Zhi). Chinese expert consensus on iruplinalkib for ALK-positive NSCLC; single-agent national guidance. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42502071/
- First Measurement: Proactively Sparing the Immune System During Stereotactic Body Radiation Therapy to Early-Stage Lung Cancer-A Randomized Phase II Study. (Int J Radiat Oncol Biol Phys). Single-institution randomized phase 2 (n=51) showing immune-organ dose-sparing SBRT reduced doses to lymph nodes/marrow in early-stage NSCLC, with lymphocyte endpoints; translational, small. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42486309/
- Efficacy and biomarker exploration of anlotinib plus penpulimab in second-line ES-SCLC: Results from the phase 2 ALTER-L041 trial. (Cell Rep Med). Single-arm multicentre phase 2 (ALTER-L041, n=65) of penpulimab plus anlotinib in platinum-resistant ES-SCLC: ORR 41.5%, median OS 10.5 months; uncontrolled. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42486095/
- Quitting Smoking and Two-Year Survival After a Diagnosis of Small-Cell Lung Cancer. (Clin Lung Cancer). Prospective UK multicentre cohort (n=720 SCLC) with CO-verified smoking status: no 2-year survival difference by baseline smoking, non-significant trend favouring quitting in limited stage (HR 0.36); small limited-stage subgroup. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42480249/
- Screening Eligibility and Survival Among Patients With Lung Cancer in Korea. (JAMA Netw Open). Nationwide Korean population cohort assessing proportion and survival of lung cancer patients by screening eligibility, highlighting cases outside smoking-based criteria; observational. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42479434/
- Design-Outcome Concordance in Targeted-Therapy trials in Non-Small cell lung cancer: a Meta-Research study. (J Natl Cancer Inst). Meta-research review of phase III RCTs in oncogene-driven NSCLC showing observed HRs exceeded design assumptions (pooled observed/expected HR ratio 0.76); methodological, not practice-changing. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42477876/
- Early Integrated Neuropsychological Care for Patients With Brain Metastases: A Randomized Controlled Trial (ATHENA Trial). (Int J Radiat Oncol Biol Phys). Phase 2 RCT of early neuropsychological care in brain metastases (43% lung primary); primary QOL endpoint not met. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42471047/
- Chemoimmunotherapy Versus Chemotherapy for Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer in the Real-World Setting: Retrospective Study of 904 Patients. (Clin Lung Cancer). Large real-world retrospective cohort (n=904) of ES-SCLC showing OS advantage of chemoimmunotherapy over chemotherapy, consistent with phase 3 data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42462290/
- Efficacy and safety of first-line chemoimmunotherapy versus chemotherapy alone for advanced pulmonary lymphoepithelioma-like carcinoma: a systematic review and real-world cohort study. (Front Immunol). Systematic review plus multicentre real-world cohort in rare pulmonary lymphoepithelioma-like carcinoma favouring first-line chemoimmunotherapy (pooled PFS HR 0.42, OS HR 0.51); non-randomised data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42459654/
- Efficacy and safety of PD-1/PD-L1 inhibitor-combined chemotherapy versus chemotherapy alone in extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer: a meta-analysis. (J Chemother). Meta-analysis of 23 studies (10 RCTs, 13 real-world; n=6364) confirming OS/PFS benefit of ICI plus chemotherapy in ES-SCLC; adds little beyond existing RCTs. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42458958/
- Real-world serplulimab plus chemotherapy for extensive-stage small cell lung cancer in china: an external validation of ASTRUM-005 trial. (BMC Med). Large real-world study with MAIC externally validating ASTRUM-005 serplulimab plus chemotherapy in ES-SCLC in China. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42458439/
- Erdafitinib in Patients With Advanced Solid Tumors With FGFR Alterations (RAGNAR): Results of Tumor-Specific Analyses and Secondary Cohorts. (JCO Precis Oncol). Phase II RAGNAR tumour-specific analyses of erdafitinib in FGFR-altered solid tumours; tumour-agnostic, lung-specific data limited in abstract. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42456086/
- [Expert Consensus on Precision Management of Pulmonary Nodules (2026 Version)]. (Zhongguo Fei Ai Za Zhi). Chinese expert consensus (2026) on precision management of pulmonary nodules; regional consensus without new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42452861/
- Optimal drug regimens for squamous non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and a Bayesian network meta-analysis of randomized trials. (J Thorac Dis). Bayesian network meta-analysis of RCTs in first-line squamous NSCLC favouring paclitaxel-based chemotherapy plus PD-(L)1 inhibitors. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42444870/
- Divergent Tumor Immune Microenvironment and Response to Neoadjuvant Chemoimmunotherapy in Resectable Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Single-Arm Phase II Trial. (Clin Cancer Res). Single-arm phase II trial of neoadjuvant pembrolizumab plus chemotherapy with spatial immune profiling identifying response-associated phenotypes; small, exploratory biomarker work. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42440361/
- Efficacy and safety of Programmed Death Ligand 1 inhibitors versus Programmed Death 1 inhibitors in the first-line treatment of advanced non-small cell lung cancer: a meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials. (PeerJ). Meta-analysis of first-line RCTs comparing PD-L1 vs PD-1 inhibitors in advanced NSCLC; indirect comparison. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42433386/
- Impact of Prior Systemic Chemotherapy on Response to ROS1 Tyrosine Kinase Inhibitors in ROS1-Rearranged Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (JCO Precis Oncol). Study-level meta-analysis suggesting prior chemotherapy is associated with lower response to ROS1 TKIs; exploratory, aggregate data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42430698/
- A systematic review and meta-analysis on survival, pathological response, and safety of durvalumab in early-stage non-small cell lung cancer. (Clin Transl Oncol). Meta-analysis of durvalumab-based perioperative treatment in early NSCLC showing increased pCR/mPR with favourable survival trends; heterogeneous studies. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42430099/
- Long-term follow-up of a phase 1/2 trial of anti-GDF-15 antibody visugromab plus anti-PD-1 antibody nivolumab in anti-PD-1/-L1 relapsed/refractory solid tumors. (J Hematol Oncol). Long-term follow-up of phase 1/2a GDFATHER-01 trial of visugromab plus nivolumab in anti-PD-(L)1-refractory tumours including non-squamous NSCLC, with durable responses; early-phase, small cohorts. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42426863/
- Adjuvant chemotherapy intensity and prognosis in completely resected high-grade neuroendocrine lung carcinoma: a post hoc analysis of JCOG1205/1206. (Lung Cancer). Post hoc analysis of phase III JCOG1205/1206 (n=217) finding higher adjuvant chemotherapy intensity not associated with better RFS in resected high-grade neuroendocrine lung carcinoma. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42425045/
- An Optimization Randomized Clinical Trial to Identify an Effective, Efficient Smoking Cessation Intervention in the Context of Lung Cancer Screening: Cessation and Screening to Save Lives. (Chest). Optimisation RCT of smoking cessation components in the lung cancer screening setting identifying combination NRT plus brief messaging as value-efficient. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42419585/
- Real-World Implementation of Tarlatamab-Dlle Therapy for Patients With Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer and Other High-Grade Neuroendocrine Neoplasms. (JCO Oncol Pract). Real-world outpatient tarlatamab programme in relapsed SCLC (n~49) with grade 3 CRS 4.1%, median PFS 3.7 months; single-programme experience. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42418753/
- Cost-Effectiveness of Universal Low-Dose Computed Tomographic Lung Cancer Screening in Singapore. (JAMA Netw Open). Model-based cost-effectiveness analysis of expanding LDCT screening to never smokers in Singapore. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42418200/
- Single-Encounter Augmented Reality-Guided Localization for Resection of Suspected Early-Stage Lung Cancer: A Randomized Clinical Trial. (JAMA Surg). Multicentre RCT showing augmented-reality-guided nodule localisation noninferior to CT-guided localisation with less radiation and pain; procedural endpoint. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42418181/
- Transportability of tislelizumab trial outcomes from RATIONALE-315, RATIONALE-303, and RATIONALE-312 to the European population in early-stage non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC), advanced NSCLC and extensive-stage small cell lung cancer (ES-SCLC). (Future Oncol). Transportability analysis projecting RATIONALE-315/303/312 tislelizumab efficacy to European populations; model-based, assumption-dependent. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42412689/
- Alirocumab Plus Cemiplimab in Immunorefractory NSCLC: A Single Arm Phase 2 Study. (Res Sq). Single-arm phase II trial of alirocumab (PCSK9 inhibitor) plus cemiplimab in immunorefractory NSCLC with responses linked to PIK3CA/PTEN/AKT1 alterations; preprint, not peer reviewed, small. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42396524/
- Anti-PD-(L)1 plus chemotherapy in advanced PD-L1-negative nonsquamous non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Immunotherapy). Meta-analysis of 12 trials (n=2084) showing anti-PD-(L)1 plus chemotherapy improves PFS (HR 0.68) and OS (HR 0.81) in PD-L1-negative nonsquamous NSCLC; subgroup-level data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42394449/
- Clinical Validation of Tissue and Liquid Companion Diagnostics for BRAF V600E Detection in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancers from the PHAROS Study. (Cancer Res Commun). Clinical bridging study from phase II PHAROS validating tissue and liquid companion diagnostics for BRAF V600E NSCLC treated with encorafenib plus binimetinib. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42391424/
- Philippine Clinical Practice Guidelines for Periodic Health Examination: Screening for Neoplastic Diseases. (Acta Med Philipp). Philippine national guidelines for cancer screening in periodic health examinations; multi-cancer, lung-specific recommendations not detailed in abstract. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42382936/
- Matching-Adjusted Indirect Comparison of Tarlatamab for Patients with Platinum-Refractory or Platinum-Resistant Extensive-Stage Small-Cell Lung Cancer. (Adv Ther). Matching-adjusted indirect comparison using DeLLphi-304 data suggesting tarlatamab improves OS vs paclitaxel and CAV in platinum-resistant ES-SCLC; residual confounding. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42377743/
- Recruitment strategies for lung cancer screening; a randomised trial in the 4-IN-THE-LUNG-RUN study. (Lung Cancer). Randomised trial within 4-IN-THE-LUNG-RUN comparing paper, online and tailored invitations for lung cancer screening recruitment. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42372381/
- European Cancer Prevention Organization on cancer screening: an expert panel position paper. (Eur J Cancer Prev). European Cancer Prevention Organization expert position paper on cancer screening across sites; lung recommendations not detailed in abstract. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42366611/
- Development and validation of a multiancestry and multitrait polygenic risk score for lung cancer. (Nat Commun). Large multiancestry GWAS meta-analysis and validated polygenic risk score for lung cancer with potential to refine screening risk stratification; no outcome data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42364979/
- The Predictive Role of ctDNA and CTCs in Patients with Advanced Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer Receiving Immunotherapy: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (Int J Mol Sci). Meta-analysis of ctDNA and CTC biomarkers predicting immunotherapy response in advanced NSCLC; heterogeneous assays. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42353230/
- Prophylactic Cranial Irradiation in MRI-Staged Limited-Stage Small-Cell Lung Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Survival and Neurocognitive Outcomes. (Cancers (Basel)). Meta-analysis in MRI-staged LS-SCLC finding PCI associated with improved OS and lower brain metastasis rate; underlying studies largely nonrandomised. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42352503/
- Comparative Efficacy of First-Line Therapeutic Options for ES-SCLC: An Indirect Comparison Using IPDfromKM-Reconstructed Individual Patient Data. (Cancers (Basel)). Indirect comparison with reconstructed IPD of first-line ICI regimens in ES-SCLC suggesting serplulimab may give the best OS estimates; hypothesis-generating. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42352404/
- Prognostic utility of circulating tumor DNA assessment in immune checkpoint inhibitor-treated advanced non-small cell lung cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (Front Immunol). Meta-analysis showing baseline ctDNA and early ctDNA dynamics are prognostic in ICI-treated advanced NSCLC; heterogeneous studies. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42344896/
- Model-Informed Development of a Subcutaneous Formulation of Tislelizumab: Phase 1 Pharmacokinetics in Patients With Cancer. (Clin Transl Sci). Phase 1 PK study supporting subcutaneous tislelizumab 300 mg thigh dosing for phase 3 evaluation; no efficacy data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42337921/
- Phase II study of 1st-line durvalumab and platinum-etoposide in advanced large-cell neuroendocrine lung carcinoma (aLCNEC). (Cancer Immunol Immunother). Small single-arm phase II trial of first-line durvalumab plus platinum-etoposide in advanced LCNEC with ORR 50% and median OS 12.8 months; underpowered. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42334618/
- Circulating Tumor DNA is Associated With Pathologic Response and Survival Outcomes in Patients With Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer Treated With Neoadjuvant Immunotherapy: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. (Arch Bronconeumol). Meta-analysis showing preoperative ctDNA clearance associated with pathologic response and EFS (HR 0.31) during neoadjuvant immunotherapy; low specificity and heterogeneity. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42331673/
- [Expert consensus on key clinical issues related to percutaneous cryoablation for lung tumors (2026 edition)]. (Zhonghua Zhong Liu Za Zhi). Chinese 2026 expert consensus on percutaneous cryoablation for lung tumours; procedural, regional consensus. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42331545/
- [Expert consensus on the multidisciplinary team management of driver gene-negative non-small cell lung cancer (2026 edition)]. (Zhonghua Zhong Liu Za Zhi). Chinese 2026 expert consensus on MDT management of driver-negative NSCLC; organisational guidance without new data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42331544/
- AI-driven body composition atlas reveals its association with NSCLC immunotherapy outcome and molecular background: a multicenter study. (NPJ Precis Oncol). Multicentre retrospective AI body composition analysis of 2132 NSCLC patients linking specific parameters to immunotherapy survival with molecular correlates; observational. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41896593/
- Sarcopenia Predicts Outcome After Chemoimmunotherapy, Not Chemotherapy, in Advanced Lung Cancer: Single-Centre Retrospective Study. (J Cachexia Sarcopenia Muscle). Single-centre retrospective study showing sarcopenia predicts worse PFS/OS with chemoimmunotherapy (adjusted HR 1.42) but not chemotherapy in advanced NSCLC; retrospective. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41877592/
- Impact of combined skeletal muscle index, subcutaneous fat index, and visceral fat index on prognosis in non-metastatic non-small cell lung cancer. (BMC Pulm Med). Large single-centre retrospective cohort (n=1661) of resected NSCLC linking low preoperative muscle and fat indices to poorer OS (all-low HR 2.99); observational body composition data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41820908/
- Association of CT-assessed sarcopenia and the development of immune-related adverse events and overall survival: a multistate survival analysis. (Transl Lung Cancer Res). Cohort multistate analysis linking CT-assessed sarcopenia to fewer irAEs and lower mortality after severe irAEs in ICI-treated NSCLC; observational body composition data. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41808702/
- Cost evaluation of a multimodal prehabilitation program for high-risk lung cancer surgery. (J Thorac Cardiovasc Surg). Cost analysis from a propensity-matched cohort (n=284) showing multimodal prehabilitation before high-risk lung surgery associated with fewer complications and net savings of CAD$5084 per patient; non-randomised. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41759946/
- Prognostic impact of sarcopenia on 5-year overall and progression-free survival in lung cancer patients: a prospective cohort study. (Front Nutr). Prospective single-centre cohort (n=403) finding AWGS-defined sarcopenia in 43% and independently associated with shorter OS (13.2 vs 43.3 months) and PFS; observational, confounding by age and PS. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41717028/
- Evaluation of the ALIBIRD mHealth Platform for Care of Patients With Lung Cancer: Prospective Pilot Study. (JMIR Cancer). Prospective pilot study of the ALIBIRD mHealth telemonitoring platform in lung cancer with usability and adherence data; very small (14 completers). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41671557/
- Long-Term Patient-Reported Outcomes after Multiport Robot-Assisted Surgery versus Video-Assisted Surgery for Lung Cancer: An Observational Cohort Study. (Ann Surg Oncol). Prospective observational cohort comparing 1-year patient-reported outcomes after robotic vs VATS lung cancer surgery; non-randomised. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41665783/
- Cancer cachexia in STK11/LKB1-mutated non-small cell lung cancer is dependent on tumor-secreted GDF15. (Nat Commun). Preclinical Nature Communications study showing tumour-secreted GDF15 drives cachexia in STK11/LKB1-mutant NSCLC models, reversible with GDF15 neutralisation; mechanistic, mouse data only. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41617691/
- Impact of vulnerability on concurrent chemoradiotherapy outcomes in patients with locally advanced non-small cell lung cancer using an integrated clinical trial database. (Ther Adv Med Oncol). Pooled analysis of Japanese clinical trial database showing clinical vulnerability, especially cachexia, associated with poorer OS after concurrent chemoradiotherapy in locally advanced NSCLC (16.5 vs 27.4 months). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41555860/
- Automated CT-derived body composition predicts pathologic response to neoadjuvant immunotherapy in non-small cell lung cancer. (Cancer Lett). Multicentre study with external validation showing AI CT-derived body composition (baseline muscle and changes) independently associated with pCR after neoadjuvant immunochemotherapy; retrospective, modest accuracy. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41506440/
- Potential five-year life years associated with stage IV reductions in NHS Galleri Trial. Preprint, not peer reviewed. Modelling of potential life-years from stage IV reductions in the NHS-Galleri MCED trial, with lung among main contributors; conditional estimate, preprint, not peer reviewed. https://www.medrxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.21.26363618v1?rss=1

- Efficacy of osimertinib combined with anti-VEGF therapy ramucirumab in managing leptomeningeal metastasis in EGFR-mutant NSCLC: a case report and literature review. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41792787/
- A radio-genomics biomarker for precision epidermal growth factor receptor mutation targeting therapy in non-small cell lung cancer. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41792401/
- MiR-29a/DNMT3A axis participates in Dihydroartemisinin's suppression on lung adenocarcinoma: Implications for overcoming acquired resistance to EGFR-TKIs. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41791429/

- Assessment of ROS1 Gene Rearrangement in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer: Concordance Between ROS1 SP384 Immunohistochemistry and ROS1 FISH Assay in a Single Center in Türkiye. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41789875/
- IL‑6: A key player in the EGFR‑TKI‑resistant tumor microenvironment and its therapeutic implications (Review). https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41789586/
- Capivasertib Combines with Trastuzumab Deruxtecan to Enhance Antitumor Activity in HER2-Positive and HER2-Low Tumors. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41789558/
- Targeting regulated cell death pathways in lung cancer: mechanisms, therapeutic strategies, and clinical translation. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41789093/
- Germ-line exon 21 EGFR V831H mutation in advanced NSCLC resistance to almonertinib: a case report. https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41788992/
